=== FILE: ./frontend/templates/Components/scripts.html ===
<script>
    // ==========================================
    // MAIN SEARCH LOGIC 
    // ==========================================
    window.allPlayersRaw = []; 

    window.setupMainSearch = function() {
        const topNavSearch = document.getElementById('playerSearch');
        const bigSearch = document.getElementById('bigPlayerSearch');
        
        if (topNavSearch) bindSearchLogic(topNavSearch, 'suggestions-list');
        if (bigSearch) bindSearchLogic(bigSearch, 'big-suggestions-list');
    };

    function bindSearchLogic(inputElement, suggestionsContainerId) {
        const suggContainer = document.getElementById(suggestionsContainerId);
        if (!suggContainer) return;

        inputElement.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            
            if (query.length < 2) {
                suggContainer.classList.add('hidden');
                return;
            }

            const matches = window.allPlayersRaw.filter(p => p.toLowerCase().includes(query));
            
            if (matches.length === 0) {
                suggContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="p-4 text-center text-slate-500 italic text-xs">No players found.</div>';
                suggContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
                return;
            }

            suggContainer.innerHTML = matches.slice(0, 10).map(p => `
                <div onclick="window.loadStats('${p.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}'); document.getElementById('${suggestionsContainerId}').classList.add('hidden');" class="px-4 py-3 hover:bg-sky-500/15 cursor-pointer text-white font-bold text-xs border-b border-white/5 last:border-0 transition-colors flex justify-between items-center group">
                    <span class="group-hover:text-sky-400 transition-colors">${p}</span>
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right text-slate-600 group-hover:text-sky-400 transition-colors text-[10px]"></i>
                </div>
            `).join('');
            
            suggContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
        });

        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target !== inputElement && e.target !== suggContainer && !suggContainer.contains(e.target)) {
                suggContainer.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });
    }

    // ==========================================
    // AUTH DROPDOWN & SMART INTERCEPTOR
    // ==========================================
    window.toggleAuthDropdown = function() {
        const menu = document.getElementById('authDropdownMenu');
        if (menu) menu.classList.toggle('hidden');
    };

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const container = document.getElementById('authDropdownContainer');
        const menu = document.getElementById('authDropdownMenu');
        if (container && menu && !container.contains(e.target)) {
            menu.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    });

    window.closeProfile = function() {
        window.switchTab('search');
    };

    window.toggleMobileProfileFilters = function() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('profileFilterDrawer');
        if (drawer) drawer.classList.toggle('hidden');
    };

    // ==========================================
    // STATS PROFILES, VOLATILITY & LEDGER LOGIC
    // ==========================================
    window.currentProfileName = "";
    window.currentProfileData = null;
    window.useRCData = false;
    
    window.setDataSource = function(source) {
        window.useRCData = source === 'rc';
        
        const btnFixtures = document.getElementById('source-btn-fixtures');
        const btnRC = document.getElementById('source-btn-rc');
        const sWrap = document.getElementById('fixtureSeasonWrap');
        const dWrap = document.getElementById('fixtureDivWrap');
        const fWrap = document.getElementById('fixtureFillinWrap');
        
        if (window.useRCData) {
            btnFixtures?.classList.remove('bg-sky-600', 'text-white');
            btnFixtures?.classList.add('text-slate-400');
            btnRC?.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
            btnRC?.classList.add('bg-sky-600', 'text-white');
            sWrap?.classList.add('hidden');
            dWrap?.classList.add('hidden');
            fWrap?.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            btnRC?.classList.remove('bg-sky-600', 'text-white');
            btnRC?.classList.add('text-slate-400');
            btnFixtures?.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
            btnFixtures?.classList.add('bg-sky-600', 'text-white');
            sWrap?.classList.remove('hidden');
            dWrap?.classList.remove('hidden');
            fWrap?.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        
        if (window.currentProfileName) {
            window.loadStats(window.currentProfileName, false);
        }
    };

    window.updateBadges = async function(playerName, activeData) {
        const gBadge = document.getElementById('gcttaRatingBadge');
        const rBadge = document.getElementById('rcRatingBadge');
        const trueBadge = document.getElementById('adminTrueRatingBadge');
        const trueText = document.getElementById('adminTrueRatingText');
        const volBadge = document.getElementById('volatilityBadge');
        const volText = document.getElementById('volatilityBadgeText');
        const divBadge = document.getElementById('playerDivBadge');

        if (activeData.home_division) {
            divBadge.innerText = `Division ${activeData.home_division}`;
            divBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else if (divBadge) {
            divBadge.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        // Update Volatility Badge (1-100)
        const volVal = activeData.vol !== undefined ? Number(activeData.vol).toFixed(0) : '25';
        const tierName = activeData.vol_tier?.tier || 'Standard Form';
        if (volText && volBadge) {
            volText.innerHTML = `VOL: ${volVal} <span class="hidden sm:inline text-[10px] opacity-80">• ${tierName}</span>`;
            volBadge.className = "font-mono font-extrabold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs border shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ";
            if (volVal <= 25) {
                volBadge.className += "bg-emerald-500/15 hover:bg-emerald-500/25 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30";
            } else if (volVal <= 55) {
                volBadge.className += "bg-sky-500/15 hover:bg-sky-500/25 text-sky-300 border-sky-500/30";
            } else if (volVal <= 80) {
                volBadge.className += "bg-amber-500/15 hover:bg-amber-500/25 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30";
            } else {
                volBadge.className += "bg-rose-500/15 hover:bg-rose-500/25 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/30";
            }
        }

        // Admin-Only True Rating Visibility
        if (activeData.is_admin_view && activeData.true_rating !== undefined && trueBadge && trueText) {
            trueText.innerText = `TRUE: ${activeData.true_rating}`;
            trueBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
            trueBadge.classList.add('flex');
        } else if (trueBadge) {
            trueBadge.classList.add('hidden');
            trueBadge.classList.remove('flex');
        }

        if (window.useRCData) {
            rBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-globe text-blue-400 text-[10px]"></i><span>RC: ${activeData.rating} <span class="text-[10px] text-blue-300 font-bold ml-0.5">±${activeData.rd}</span></span>`;
            try {
                const gRes = await fetch(`/api/stats/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}`);
                if (!gRes.ok) throw new Error();
                const gData = await gRes.json();
                gBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-chart-line text-sky-400 text-[10px]"></i><span>GCTTA: ${gData.rating} <span class="text-[10px] text-sky-300 font-bold ml-0.5">±${gData.rd}</span></span>`;
            } catch(e) {
                gBadge.innerHTML = "GCTTA: N/A";
            }
        } else {
            gBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-chart-line text-sky-400 text-[10px]"></i><span>GCTTA: ${activeData.rating} <span class="text-[10px] text-sky-300 font-bold ml-0.5">±${activeData.rd}</span></span>`;
            try {
                const rRes = await fetch(`/api/stats/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}?source=rc`);
                if (!rRes.ok) throw new Error();
                const rData = await rRes.json();
                rBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-globe text-blue-400 text-[10px]"></i><span>RC: ${rData.rating} <span class="text-[10px] text-blue-300 font-bold ml-0.5">±${rData.rd}</span></span>`;
            } catch(e) {
                rBadge.innerHTML = `<span class="opacity-60">RC: Unlinked</span>`;
            }
        }
    };

    window.openVolatilityGuideModal = function() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('volatilityGuideModal');
        const banner = document.getElementById('playerCurrentVolatilityBanner');
        if (!modal) return;

        if (window.currentProfileData && banner) {
            const d = window.currentProfileData;
            const vol = d.vol !== undefined ? Number(d.vol).toFixed(1) : '25.0';
            const tier = d.vol_tier || { tier: 'Standard Form', description: 'Typical competitive club form.' };
            banner.innerHTML = `
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                    <span class="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">${d.name}'s Volatility</span>
                    <span class="font-mono font-black text-base text-white">${vol} / 100</span>
                </div>
                <div class="text-xs font-bold text-sky-400">${tier.tier}</div>
                <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1 leading-relaxed">${tier.description}</p>
            `;
        }
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    };

    window.showRatingHistory = async function(playerName, source = 'gctta') {
        const modal = document.getElementById('ratingHistoryModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('historyContent');
        const subtitle = document.getElementById('ledgerSubtitle');
        
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-8"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl mb-2"></i><br>Loading ledger...</div>';
        subtitle.innerText = source === 'rc' ? 'Ratings Central Match Ledger' : 'GCTTA Rating, SD & Decay Ledger';
        
        try {
            const sourceParam = source === 'rc' ? '?source=rc' : '';
            const res = await fetch(`/api/rating_history/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}${sourceParam}`);
            const history = await res.json();
            
            if (!history || history.length === 0) {
                content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-8 text-xs font-bold">No historical ledger entries recorded yet.</div>';
                return;
            }

            content.innerHTML = history.map(h => {
                const isDecay = h.is_decay;
                const isCheckpoint = h.opponent === 'Admin Calibration';
                
                const rDeltaNum = Number(h.rating_change || 0);
                const rDelta = rDeltaNum > 0 ? `+${rDeltaNum.toFixed(1)}` : rDeltaNum.toFixed(1);
                const rColor = rDeltaNum > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : (rDeltaNum < 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-slate-500');
                
                const sdDeltaNum = Number(h.sd_change || 0);
                const sdDelta = sdDeltaNum > 0 ? `+${sdDeltaNum.toFixed(1)}` : sdDeltaNum.toFixed(1);
                const sdColor = sdDeltaNum > 0 ? 'text-amber-400' : (sdDeltaNum < 0 ? 'text-sky-400' : 'text-slate-500');

                const hasTrue = h.true_rating !== undefined;
                const tDeltaNum = Number(h.true_rating_change || 0);
                const tDelta = tDeltaNum > 0 ? `+${tDeltaNum.toFixed(1)}` : tDeltaNum.toFixed(1);

                let rowBorder = 'border-white/5';
                let titleHtml = `<span class="text-white font-bold text-xs">vs ${h.opponent || 'Opponent'}</span>`;

                if (isDecay) {
                    rowBorder = 'border-amber-500/25 bg-amber-500/5';
                    titleHtml = `<span class="text-amber-300 font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left text-amber-400"></i> ${h.opponent}</span>`;
                } else if (isCheckpoint) {
                    rowBorder = 'border-purple-500/30 bg-purple-500/10';
                    titleHtml = `<span class="text-purple-300 font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5"><i class="fa-solid fa-lock text-purple-400"></i> Admin Calibration Checkpoint</span>`;
                }

                return `
                <div class="p-3 bg-black/40 rounded-xl border ${rowBorder} flex items-center justify-between gap-2 hover:border-white/20 transition-colors">
                    <div class="min-w-0">
                        <div class="truncate mb-0.5">${titleHtml}</div>
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">${h.date} ${h.result_str ? `• <span class="text-slate-300 font-bold">${h.result_str}</span>` : ''}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
                        <div class="text-right">
                            <div class="text-sky-400 font-mono font-black text-sm leading-none">${h.rating}</div>
                            <div class="text-[10px] ${rColor} font-mono font-bold mt-1">${rDeltaNum !== 0 ? rDelta : '0.0'}</div>
                        </div>
                        ${hasTrue ? `
                        <div class="text-right border-l border-white/10 pl-2.5">
                            <div class="text-purple-300 font-mono font-black text-xs leading-none" title="True Rating">T: ${h.true_rating}</div>
                            <div class="text-[10px] text-purple-400 font-mono font-bold mt-1">${tDeltaNum !== 0 ? tDelta : '0.0'}</div>
                        </div>
                        ` : ''}
                        <div class="text-right border-l border-white/10 pl-2.5 w-14">
                            <div class="text-slate-300 font-mono font-bold text-xs leading-none">±${h.sd}</div>
                            <div class="text-[10px] ${sdColor} font-mono font-bold mt-1">${sdDeltaNum !== 0 ? sdDelta : '-'}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
        } catch(e) {
            content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-rose-400 text-center py-8 text-xs font-bold">Failed to load history ledger.</div>';
        }
    };

    window.showMatchDetails = function(date, opponent, result, score, delta) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('matchDetailsModal');
        if(!modal) return;
        const formattedScore = (score && score !== 'null' && score !== 'undefined') ? score : 'Score Not Recorded';
        const formattedDelta = (delta && delta !== 0 && delta !== 'null') ? (delta > 0 ? '+' + Number(delta).toFixed(1) : Number(delta).toFixed(1)) : '0.0';
        const deltaColor = delta > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : (delta < 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-slate-400');
        
        document.getElementById('matchDetailsContent').innerHTML = `
            <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase tracking-widest font-extrabold">${date}</div>
            <div class="text-xl font-black text-white mb-5">${result} vs <span class="text-sky-400">${opponent}</span></div>
            <div class="bg-black/50 rounded-xl p-4 border border-white/10 mb-4">
                <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-extrabold uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Set / Game Score</div>
                <div class="text-2xl font-mono font-black text-white">${formattedScore}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="text-xs font-mono font-extrabold ${deltaColor} bg-black/40 py-2 px-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10 inline-block">Rating Shift: ${formattedDelta}</div>
        `;
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    };

    window.loadStats = async function(playerName, pushHistory = true) {
        if (!playerName) return;
        window.currentProfileName = playerName;
        if (pushHistory) {
            history.pushState({ page: 'profile', name: playerName }, "", "#profile/" + encodeURIComponent(playerName));
        }
        
        ['home', 'week', 'sheets', 'search', 'notices', 'community', 'live-center', 'rules', 'player-hub'].forEach(t => { 
            const el = document.getElementById(`tab-${t}`); 
            if(el) el.classList.add('hidden'); 
        });
        
        const statsContainer = document.getElementById('statsContainer');
        if (statsContainer) {
            statsContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
            statsContainer.classList.add('animate-fade-in');
        }

        document.getElementById('playerName').innerText = playerName;
        
        const season = document.getElementById('seasonSelect')?.value || 'Career';
        const division = document.getElementById('profileDivisionSelect')?.value || 'All';
        const sourceParam = window.useRCData ? '&source=rc' : '';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/stats/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}?season=${encodeURIComponent(season)}&division=${encodeURIComponent(division)}${sourceParam}`);
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Player not found");
            const d = await res.json();
            window.currentProfileData = d;
            
            window.updateBadges(playerName, d);
            window.renderProfile();
        } catch(e) {
            window.currentProfileData = null;
            document.getElementById('gcttaRatingBadge').innerHTML = "GCTTA: N/A";
            document.getElementById('rcRatingBadge').innerHTML = "RC: N/A";
            document.getElementById('matchHistory').innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-rose-400 text-xs font-bold">Error loading profile data.</div>';
        }
    };

    window.renderProfile = function() {
        if (!window.currentProfileData) return;
        const d = window.currentProfileData;
        
        const toggleEl = document.getElementById('profileFillinToggle');
        const isCombined = toggleEl ? toggleEl.checked : true; 
        
        const toggleBg = document.getElementById('toggleBg');
        const toggleDot = document.getElementById('toggleDot');
        
        if (toggleBg && toggleDot) {
            if (isCombined) {
                toggleBg.classList.remove('bg-slate-700');
                toggleBg.classList.add('bg-sky-500');
                toggleDot.classList.add('translate-x-4');
            } else {
                toggleBg.classList.remove('bg-sky-500');
                toggleBg.classList.add('bg-slate-700');
                toggleDot.classList.remove('translate-x-4');
            }
        }
        
        const displayStats = isCombined ? d.combined : d.regular; 
        
        document.getElementById('matchesPlayed').innerText = displayStats.matches || 0;
        document.getElementById('wins').innerText = displayStats.wins || 0;
        document.getElementById('losses').innerText = displayStats.losses || 0;
        document.getElementById('winRate').innerText = displayStats.win_rate || "0%";
        
        window.renderInsights();

        const histContainer = document.getElementById('matchHistory');
        if (!displayStats.match_history || displayStats.match_history.length === 0) {
            histContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="p-10 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs font-semibold">No matches found for this filter.</div>';
            return;
        }

        histContainer.innerHTML = displayStats.match_history.map(m => {
            const won = m.result === 'Win';
            const borderLeft = won ? 'border-l-4 border-l-emerald-500' : 'border-l-4 border-l-rose-500';
            const textColor = won ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400';
            
            const delta = Number(m.delta || 0);
            const deltaStr = (delta > 0 ? '+' : '') + delta.toFixed(1);
            const deltaColor = delta > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : (delta < 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-slate-500');
            
            const fillBadge = m.type === 'Fill-in'
                ? `<span class="text-[9px] bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider">Fill-in</span>`
                : '';
            const divLabel = m.division && m.division !== 'Unknown'
                ? `<span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold">${m.division}</span>`
                : '';

            let detailsHtml = m.details
                ? `<div class="mt-2 text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-black/40 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-white/5 inline-block">${m.details}</div>`
                : '';
            
            return `
            <div class="p-3.5 md:p-4 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0 hover:bg-white/[0.03] transition-colors ${borderLeft}">
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">
                    <div class="min-w-0">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1">
                            <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">${m.date}</span>
                            ${divLabel}
                            ${fillBadge}
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-sm md:text-base text-white font-bold truncate">
                            <span class="text-slate-500 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase mr-1">vs</span>
                            <span class="hover:text-sky-400 cursor-pointer transition-colors" onclick="window.loadStats('${m.opponent.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">${m.opponent}</span>
                        </div>
                        ${detailsHtml}
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0 cursor-pointer" onclick="window.showMatchDetails('${m.date}', '${m.opponent.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${m.result}', '${m.details || m.score || ''}', ${delta})">
                        <div class="text-right">
                            <div class="font-mono font-black text-base ${textColor} leading-none">${m.score}</div>
                            <div class="${deltaColor} text-[10px] font-mono font-bold mt-1">${deltaStr}</div>
                        </div>
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right text-[10px] text-slate-600"></i>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>`;
        }).join('');
    };

    window.switchProfileTab = function(tabId) {
        document.getElementById('profile-tab-history').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('profile-tab-insights').classList.add('hidden');
        
        const btnHist = document.getElementById('tab-btn-history');
        const btnIns = document.getElementById('tab-btn-insights');

        btnHist.className = "flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2";
        btnIns.className = "flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2";

        document.getElementById('profile-tab-' + tabId).classList.remove('hidden');
        const activeBtn = document.getElementById('tab-btn-' + tabId);
        activeBtn.className = "flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider bg-sky-600 text-white shadow transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2";

        if (tabId === 'insights') window.renderInsights();
    };

    window.openCompareModal = function() {
        if (!window.currentProfileName) return;
        document.getElementById('h2h-p1-name').innerText = window.currentProfileName;
        document.getElementById('h2h-p1-hidden').value = window.currentProfileName;
        
        const h2hSearch = document.getElementById('h2h-search');
        const h2hSugg = document.getElementById('h2h-suggestions');
        
        const newSearch = h2hSearch.cloneNode(true);
        h2hSearch.parentNode.replaceChild(newSearch, h2hSearch);
        
        newSearch.value = '';
        document.getElementById('h2h-results-area').innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-12 text-slate-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass text-2xl mb-2"></i><p class="font-bold text-xs">Search for an opponent to compare head-to-head.</p></div>';
        
        newSearch.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const v = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            h2hSugg.innerHTML = '';
            if (v.length < 2) { h2hSugg.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
            
            const matches = window.allPlayersRaw.filter(p => p.toLowerCase().includes(v) && p !== window.currentProfileName).slice(0, 6);
            if (matches.length > 0) {
                h2hSugg.classList.remove('hidden');
                matches.forEach(m => {
                    let div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = "px-4 py-2.5 hover:bg-sky-500/20 cursor-pointer text-white font-bold text-xs border-b border-white/5";
                    div.innerText = m;
                    div.onclick = () => {
                        newSearch.value = m;
                        h2hSugg.classList.add('hidden');
                        runCompare(window.currentProfileName, m);
                    };
                    h2hSugg.appendChild(div);
                });
            } else {
                h2hSugg.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });
        
        document.getElementById('compareModal').classList.remove('hidden');
        setTimeout(() => newSearch.focus(), 100);
    };
    window.closeCompareModal = () => document.getElementById('compareModal').classList.add('hidden');

    async function runCompare(p1, p2) {
        const resultsArea = document.getElementById('h2h-results-area');
        resultsArea.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-12 text-sky-400"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl mb-2"></i><p class="font-bold text-xs">Simulating matchup...</p></div>';
        
        try {
            const sourceParam = window.useRCData ? '&source=rc' : '';
            const res = await fetch(`/api/h2h?p1=${encodeURIComponent(p1)}&p2=${encodeURIComponent(p2)}${sourceParam}`);
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
            
            const p1Lower = p1.toLowerCase();
            const historyHtml = data.history && data.history.length > 0 
                ? data.history.map(m => {
                    const p1IsMatchP1 = m.p1.toLowerCase() === p1Lower;
                    const ourP1Score = p1IsMatchP1 ? m.s1 : m.s2;
                    const ourP2Score = p1IsMatchP1 ? m.s2 : m.s1;
                    const isP1Win = ourP1Score > ourP2Score;
                    return `
                    <div class="bg-black/40 p-3 rounded-xl border ${isP1Win ? 'border-sky-500/25' : 'border-rose-500/25'} mb-2 flex justify-between items-center">
                        <div>
                            <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">${m.date}</div>
                            <div class="text-white font-bold text-xs mt-0.5">${isP1Win ? `<span class="text-sky-400">${p1}</span> won` : `<span class="text-rose-400">${p2}</span> won`}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="font-mono font-black ${isP1Win ? 'text-sky-400' : 'text-rose-400'} text-sm bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-white/5">${ourP1Score}-${ourP2Score}</div>
                    </div>`;
                }).join('')
                : '<div class="text-center py-6 text-slate-500 text-xs border border-white/5 bg-black/20 rounded-xl">No previous encounters recorded.</div>';

            resultsArea.innerHTML = `
                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-4">
                    <div class="bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-center">
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 truncate">${p1} Wins</div>
                        <div class="text-3xl font-black text-sky-400 font-mono">${data.p1_wins}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-center">
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 truncate">${p2} Wins</div>
                        <div class="text-3xl font-black text-rose-400 font-mono">${data.p2_wins}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <div class="bg-black/40 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-4 mb-5">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 text-xs font-mono font-bold">
                        <span class="text-white">${data.p1_rating} <span class="text-slate-500">±${data.p1_rd}</span></span>
                        <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-sans font-extrabold tracking-widest">Win Probability</span>
                        <span class="text-white">${data.p2_rating} <span class="text-slate-500">±${data.p2_rd}</span></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex justify-between text-xs font-black mb-1.5 font-mono">
                        <span class="text-sky-400">${data.p1_prob}% ($${data.p1_odds})</span>
                        <span class="text-rose-400">${data.p2_prob}% ($${data.p2_odds})</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="w-full bg-slate-800 h-2.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex">
                        <div class="bg-sky-500 h-full" style="width: ${data.p1_prob}%"></div>
                        <div class="bg-rose-500 h-full" style="width: ${data.p2_prob}%"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <h3 class="text-slate-400 font-extrabold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Previous Encounters</h3>
                <div class="space-y-1.5">${historyHtml}</div>
            `;
        } catch(e) {
            resultsArea.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-12 text-rose-400 text-xs font-bold">Could not load comparison data.</div>`;
        }
    }

    window.renderInsights = function() {
        const loading = document.getElementById('insightsLoading');
        const content = document.getElementById('insightsContent');
        
        if (!window.currentProfileData) return;
        const d = window.currentProfileData;
        const insights = d.insights || {};
        const totalMatches = d.career_matches || 0;
        
        loading.classList.add('hidden');
        content.classList.remove('hidden');

        if (totalMatches === 0) {
            content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-xs py-10">Not enough matches for insights.</div>';
            return;
        }

        function renderInsightCard(title, icon, dataArray, desc) {
            dataArray = dataArray || [];
            const count = dataArray.length;
            const pct = totalMatches > 0 ? ((count / totalMatches) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0%';
            const preview = dataArray.slice(0, 3).map(m => `
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center py-1.5 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0 text-xs">
                    <span class="text-slate-300 font-semibold truncate pr-2">${m.opponent}</span>
                    <span class="${m.result.includes('Win') ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'} font-mono font-bold shrink-0">${m.result}</span>
                </div>
            `).join('');

            return `
            <div class="bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div>
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                        <span class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sky-400 flex items-center gap-1.5"><i class="fa-solid ${icon}"></i> ${title}</span>
                        <span class="font-mono font-black text-lg text-white">${count} <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-normal">(${pct})</span></span>
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mb-3">${desc}</p>
                </div>
                ${count > 0 ? `<div class="bg-black/40 rounded-lg p-2.5 border border-white/5">${preview}</div>` : '<div class="text-[11px] text-slate-600 italic">None recorded yet</div>'}
            </div>`;
        }

        content.innerHTML = `
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
                ${renderInsightCard('Clean Sweeps', 'fa-broom', insights.sweeps, 'Matches won without dropping a single set.')}
                ${renderInsightCard('Clutch Close Calls', 'fa-fire', insights.close_calls, 'Matches decided in the final possible set.')}
                ${renderInsightCard('Heavy Swings', 'fa-bolt', insights.big_swings, 'Matches with major rating shifts (±45+ pts).')}
                ${renderInsightCard('Blowouts', 'fa-wind', insights.blowouts, 'Matches lost in straight sets.')}
            </div>
        `;
    };

    // ==========================================
    // STANDINGS / LEADERBOARDS (FIXED SORTING)
    // ==========================================
    window.loadDivisionLeaderboard = async function() {
        const season = document.getElementById('boardSeason')?.value;
        const division = document.getElementById('boardDiv')?.value;
        const list = document.getElementById('leaderboardContent');
        
        if (!list || !season || !division) return;
        
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-16 text-sky-400"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-3xl mb-3"></i><p class="text-slate-400 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">Loading standings...</p></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/rankings/${encodeURIComponent(season)}/${encodeURIComponent(division)}`);
            let rankings = await res.json();
            
            rankings = rankings.filter(r => r.regular && r.regular.matches > 0);
            
            rankings.sort((a, b) => {
                const winRateA = a.regular.matches > 0 ? (a.regular.wins / a.regular.matches) : 0;
                const winRateB = b.regular.matches > 0 ? (b.regular.wins / b.regular.matches) : 0;
                if (winRateB !== winRateA) return winRateB - winRateA;
                return b.regular.wins - a.regular.wins;
            });
            
            if (rankings.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 mt-6 bg-black/30 p-8 rounded-xl border border-white/10 text-xs font-bold">No regular season matches recorded for this division yet.</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            list.innerHTML = rankings.map((r, index) => {
                const winRate = Math.round((r.regular.wins / r.regular.matches) * 100);
                const isTop3 = index < 3;
                
                let rankBadge = `<div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-black/50 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-black text-xs border border-white/10 shrink-0">${index + 1}</div>`;
                if(index === 0) rankBadge = `<div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-yellow-500 text-black flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs shrink-0 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(234,179,8,0.4)]"><i class="fa-solid fa-trophy"></i></div>`;
                if(index === 1) rankBadge = `<div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-slate-300 text-black flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-black text-xs shrink-0">2</div>`;
                if(index === 2) rankBadge = `<div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-amber-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-black text-xs shrink-0">3</div>`;
                
                const trueBadge = r.true_rating !== undefined
                    ? `<span class="text-purple-400 ml-1.5">• T:${r.true_rating}</span>`
                    : '';

                return `
                <div onclick="window.loadStats('${r.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="glass-panel p-3 md:p-3.5 rounded-xl border ${isTop3 ? 'border-sky-500/30' : 'border-white/10'} mb-2 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:border-sky-500/50 transition group">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 min-w-0">
                        ${rankBadge}
                        <div class="min-w-0 pr-2">
                            <div class="font-extrabold text-white text-sm group-hover:text-sky-400 transition-colors truncate">${r.name}</div>
                            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <span class="text-sky-400 font-bold">${r.rating_val}</span>
                                <span class="text-slate-500">±${r.sigma}</span>
                                ${trueBadge}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-4 shrink-0">
                        <div class="text-right">
                            <div class="text-white font-mono font-bold text-xs">${r.regular.wins}W - ${r.regular.losses}L</div>
                            <div class="text-[10px] font-mono ${winRate >= 50 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'} font-extrabold">${winRate}%</div>
                        </div>
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right text-slate-600 text-[10px]"></i>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
        } catch(e) {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-rose-400 text-xs font-bold">Error loading standings.</div>';
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // MATCH CENTRE (Results Tab)
    // ==========================================
    window.loadResults = async function() {
        const season = document.getElementById('resultsSeason')?.value;
        const week = document.getElementById('resultsWeek')?.value || '1';
        const list = document.getElementById('resultsContainer');
        
        if (!list || !season) return;
        
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-2 text-center py-16 text-sky-400"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-3xl mb-3"></i><p class="text-slate-400 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">Loading matches...</p></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/week/${encodeURIComponent(season)}/${encodeURIComponent(week)}`);
            const matches = await res.json();
            
            if (matches.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-2 text-center text-slate-500 mt-4 bg-black/30 p-8 rounded-xl border border-white/10 text-xs font-bold">No matches recorded for Week ${week}.</div>`;
                return;
            }
            
            list.innerHTML = matches.map(m => {
                let s1 = 0, s2 = 0;
                if (m.score && m.score.includes('-')) {
                    const parts = m.score.split('-');
                    s1 = parseInt(parts[0]) || 0;
                    s2 = parseInt(parts[1]) || 0;
                }
                const p1Won = s1 > s2;
                const p2Won = s2 > s1;
                const p1Fill = m.p1_fill ? `<span class="text-[8px] bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ml-1 uppercase font-extrabold">Sub</span>` : '';
                const p2Fill = m.p2_fill ? `<span class="text-[8px] bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded mr-1 uppercase font-extrabold">Sub</span>` : '';
                
                return `
                <div class="glass-panel p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 hover:border-white/20 transition-colors">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-2.5 pb-2 border-b border-white/5">
                        <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-sky-400 bg-sky-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-sky-500/20">${m.division || 'Fixtures'}</span>
                        <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">${m.date || ''}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-[1fr,auto,1fr] gap-3 items-center">
                        <div class="text-right cursor-pointer truncate" onclick="window.loadStats('${m.p1.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
                            <span class="text-xs md:text-sm font-extrabold ${p1Won ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-400'} hover:text-sky-400 transition-colors">${m.p1}</span>${p1Fill}
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-white/10 font-mono font-black text-sm">
                            <span class="${p1Won ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-slate-300'}">${s1}</span>
                            <span class="text-slate-600 mx-1.5">-</span>
                            <span class="${p2Won ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-slate-300'}">${s2}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-left cursor-pointer truncate" onclick="window.loadStats('${m.p2.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
                            ${p2Fill}<span class="text-xs md:text-sm font-extrabold ${p2Won ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-400'} hover:text-sky-400 transition-colors">${m.p2}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
        } catch(e) {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-2 text-center py-10 text-rose-400 text-xs font-bold">Error loading match results.</div>';
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // NOTICES, FORUM & DONORS
    // ==========================================
    window.loadNotices = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('noticesFeed');
        if(!list) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/notices');
            const notices = await res.json();
            if(notices.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-8 text-xs">No recent notices.</div>';
                return;
            }
            list.innerHTML = notices.map(n => {
                let badgeClass = 'border-white/10 bg-black/30';
                if(n.type === 'Update') badgeClass = 'border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-950/20';
                if(n.type === 'Event') badgeClass = 'border-yellow-500/30 bg-yellow-950/20';
                if(n.type === 'Alert') badgeClass = 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-950/20';
                return `
                <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border ${badgeClass} mb-3 shadow">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                        <h4 class="font-black text-sm text-white">${n.title}</h4>
                        <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 shrink-0 ml-2">${n.date_str}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">${n.message}</p>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.loadCommunityFeed = async function() {
        const feed = document.getElementById('communityFeed');
        if(!feed) return;
        feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-sky-400"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/hub/forum');
            const posts = await res.json();
            if(posts.length === 0) {
                feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 py-10 text-xs">No posts yet.</div>';
                return;
            }
            feed.innerHTML = posts.map(p => `
                <div class="glass-panel p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10 mb-3">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2.5 pb-2 border-b border-white/5">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer" onclick="window.loadStats('${p.author_name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
                            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center text-sky-400 border border-white/10 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-user"></i></div>
                            <div>
                                <div class="font-bold text-white text-xs hover:text-sky-400">${p.author_name}</div>
                                <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">${p.time_str}</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-xs md:text-sm text-slate-200 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">${p.content}</div>
                    ${p.image_url ? `<img src="${p.image_url}" class="w-full rounded-xl mt-3 max-h-80 object-cover border border-white/10">` : ''}
                </div>
            `).join('');
        } catch(e) {
            feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-rose-400 text-center py-10 text-xs">Failed to load feed.</div>';
        }
    };
    
    window.loadTopDonors = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('donorsList');
        if(!list) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/top_donors');
            const donors = await res.json();
            if (donors.length === 0) return;
            list.innerHTML = donors.map((d, i) => `
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center bg-black/30 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-white/5 mb-1.5">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span class="text-xs font-mono font-black ${i === 0 ? 'text-yellow-400' : 'text-slate-500'}">#${i + 1}</span>
                        <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">${d.name}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span class="text-emerald-400 font-mono text-xs font-extrabold">$${Math.round(d.amount)}</span>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    // ==========================================
    // DONATION & STRIPE CHECKOUT LOGIC
    // ==========================================
    let stripeInstance = null;
    let stripeElements = null;

    window.openDonateModal = function() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('donateModal');
        if (modal) {
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('donate-step-1').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('donate-step-2').classList.add('hidden');
        }
    };

    window.closeDonateModal = function() {
        document.getElementById('donateModal')?.classList.add('hidden');
    };

    window.setDonationAmount = function(amt) {
        const input = document.getElementById('custom-donate-amount');
        if (input) input.value = amt;
    };

    window.proceedToPayment = async function() {
        const amt = document.getElementById('custom-donate-amount').value;
        if (!amt || amt < 5) return alert("Minimum donation is $5.");

        const btn = document.getElementById('proceed-pay-btn');
        const origText = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing...';
        btn.disabled = true;

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/create-payment-intent', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ amount: amt })
            });
            const data = await res.json();

            if (data.clientSecret) {
                document.getElementById('donate-step-1').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('donate-step-2').classList.remove('hidden');

                if (typeof Stripe !== 'undefined') {
                    if (!stripeInstance) {
                        const configRes = await fetch('/api/stripe-config');
                        const configData = await configRes.json();
                        stripeInstance = Stripe(configData.publicKey);
                    }
                    stripeElements = stripeInstance.elements({ clientSecret: data.clientSecret, appearance: { theme: 'night' } });
                    const paymentElement = stripeElements.create('payment');
                    paymentElement.mount('#payment-element');
                }
            } else {
                alert("Error: " + (data.error || 'Unknown Error'));
            }
        } catch (e) {
            alert("Network Error connecting to Stripe.");
        } finally {
            btn.innerHTML = origText;
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    window.submitStripePayment = async function() {
        if (!stripeInstance || !stripeElements) return;
        const btn = document.getElementById('final-pay-btn');
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing...';
        btn.disabled = true;

        const donorName = document.getElementById('donate-name').value || 'Anonymous';
        const donationAmount = document.getElementById('custom-donate-amount').value;

        const { error, paymentIntent } = await stripeInstance.confirmPayment({
            elements: stripeElements,
            confirmParams: { return_url: window.location.href },
            redirect: 'if_required'
        });

        if (error) {
            const msg = document.getElementById('payment-message');
            msg.innerText = error.message;
            msg.classList.remove('hidden');
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-lock"></i> Pay Securely Now';
            btn.disabled = false;
        } else if (paymentIntent && paymentIntent.status === 'succeeded') {
            await fetch('/api/record_donation', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ intent_id: paymentIntent.id, name: donorName, amount: donationAmount })
            });
            document.getElementById('donate-step-2').innerHTML = `
                <div class="text-center py-8">
                    <div class="text-emerald-400 text-4xl mb-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i></div>
                    <h3 class="text-xl font-black text-white mb-1">Thank You!</h3>
                    <button onclick="window.location.reload()" class="mt-4 bg-slate-800 text-white font-bold py-2 px-5 rounded-xl text-xs">Close</button>
                </div>`;
        }
    };
</script>

<div class="admin-view animate-fade-in max-w-4xl mx-auto">

=== FILE: ./frontend/templates/Components/Admin/scripts.html ===
<script>
    console.log("✅ Admin UI Script Loaded Successfully!");

    // Native Zero-Dependency WebAuthn Passkey Registration Helper
    function _adminB64urlToUint8Array(base64url) {
        const padding = '='.repeat((4 - (base64url.length % 4)) % 4);
        const base64 = (base64url + padding).replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
        const rawData = window.atob(base64);
        const outputArray = new Uint8Array(rawData.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < rawData.length; ++i) {
            outputArray[i] = rawData.charCodeAt(i);
        }
        return outputArray;
    }

    function _adminBufferToB64url(buffer) {
        const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
        let binary = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < bytes.byteLength; i++) {
            binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
        }
        return window.btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
    }

    window.registerAdminPasskey = async function() {
        if (window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1') {
            if (confirm("Apple Passkeys require 'localhost' instead of '127.0.0.1' when testing locally. Switch to localhost now?")) {
                window.location.href = window.location.href.replace('127.0.0.1', 'localhost');
            }
            return;
        }

        try {
            const optRes = await fetch('/api/auth/passkey/register-options', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({})
            });
            const options = await optRes.json();
            if (options.error) throw new Error(options.error);

            const publicKey = {
                challenge: _adminB64urlToUint8Array(options.challenge),
                rp: { name: options.rp.name },
                user: {
                    id: _adminB64urlToUint8Array(options.user.id),
                    name: options.user.name,
                    displayName: options.user.displayName
                },
                pubKeyCredParams: options.pubKeyCredParams,
                timeout: options.timeout || 60000,
                attestation: options.attestation || 'none',
                authenticatorSelection: options.authenticatorSelection
            };

            if (options.rp.id && options.rp.id !== '127.0.0.1') {
                publicKey.rp.id = options.rp.id;
            }

            const cred = await navigator.credentials.create({ publicKey });
            if (!cred) throw new Error("Passkey creation was cancelled.");

            const regResponse = {
                id: cred.id,
                rawId: _adminBufferToB64url(cred.rawId),
                type: cred.type,
                response: {
                    attestationObject: _adminBufferToB64url(cred.response.attestationObject),
                    clientDataJSON: _adminBufferToB64url(cred.response.clientDataJSON),
                    transports: cred.response.getTransports ? cred.response.getTransports() : ['internal', 'hybrid']
                }
            };

            const verifyRes = await fetch('/api/auth/passkey/register-verify', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ reg_response: regResponse })
            });
            const resData = await verifyRes.json();
            if (resData.success) {
                alert("✅ " + (resData.message || "Synced Passkey registered! You can now log in using Face ID / Touch ID across your Apple devices."));
            } else {
                throw new Error(resData.error || "Failed to verify passkey.");
            }
        } catch (err) {
            alert("Passkey Setup: " + (err.message || "Cancelled or failed."));
        }
    };

    window.submitChangePassword = async function() {
        const newPass = document.getElementById('newAdminPassword').value;
        const confirmPass = document.getElementById('confirmAdminPassword').value;
        
        if (!newPass || newPass.length < 6) return alert("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
        if (newPass !== confirmPass) return alert("Passwords do not match.");
        
        try {
            const btn = document.querySelector('#changePasswordModal button.bg-indigo-600');
            const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Updating...';
            btn.disabled = true;
            
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/change_password', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ password: newPass })
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            
            btn.innerHTML = originalText;
            btn.disabled = false;
            
            if (data.success) {
                alert("✅ Password successfully updated! You can now use this password for future logins.");
                document.getElementById('newAdminPassword').value = '';
                document.getElementById('confirmAdminPassword').value = '';
                document.getElementById('changePasswordModal').classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error updating password: " + data.error);
            }
        } catch(e) {
            alert("❌ System error occurred.");
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // UI NAVIGATION ENGINE
    // ==========================================
    window.setAdminCategory = function(catId) {
        ['ops', 'players', 'setup', 'comms', 'system'].forEach(c => {
            const btn = document.getElementById(`cat-${c}`);
            if (btn) { 
                btn.classList.remove('border-blue-500', 'text-blue-400'); 
                btn.classList.add('border-transparent', 'text-slate-500'); 
            }
            const sub = document.getElementById(`sub-${c}`);
            if (sub) {
                sub.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });

        const targetBtn = document.getElementById(`cat-${catId}`);
        if (targetBtn) { 
            targetBtn.classList.remove('border-transparent', 'text-slate-500'); 
            targetBtn.classList.add('border-blue-500', 'text-blue-400'); 
        }
        
        const targetSub = document.getElementById(`sub-${catId}`);
        if (targetSub) {
            targetSub.classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        if (catId === 'ops') window.switchAdminTab('live');
        if (catId === 'players') window.switchAdminTab('players');
        if (catId === 'setup') window.switchAdminTab('schedules');
        if (catId === 'comms') window.switchAdminTab('notices');
        if (catId === 'system') window.switchAdminTab('audit');
    };

    window.switchAdminTab = function(tabId) {
        const allTabs = [
            'live', 'manual', 'history', 'date-errors', 'tournaments', 
            'players', 'ratings-central', 'diagnostics', 'schedules', 'team-editor', 'broadcast', 
            'sms-inbox', 'notices', 'community-mod', 'inbox-feedback', 'donations', 'audit', 'chat',
            'pending-accounts'
        ];

        allTabs.forEach(t => {
            const section = document.getElementById(`admin-${t}`);
            if (section) section.classList.add('hidden');
            
            const btn = document.getElementById(`tab-btn-${t}`);
            if (btn) btn.className = "px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap transition-colors";
        });
        
        const targetSection = document.getElementById(`admin-${tabId}`);
        if (targetSection) targetSection.classList.remove('hidden');
        
        const targetBtn = document.getElementById(`tab-btn-${tabId}`);
        if (targetBtn) targetBtn.className = "px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-white/10 rounded-lg cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap transition-colors";

        if (tabId === 'inbox-feedback') window.loadPendingReports();
        if (tabId === 'date-errors') window.loadDateErrors();
        if (tabId === 'notices') window.loadAdminNotices();
        if (tabId === 'chat') window.loadAdminChat();
        if (tabId === 'donations') window.loadDonations();
        if (tabId === 'sms-inbox') window.loadSmsInbox();
        if (tabId === 'community-mod') window.loadCommunityMod();
        if (tabId === 'team-editor') window.loadTeams();
        if (tabId === 'pending-accounts') window.loadPendingAccounts();
        
        if (tabId === 'history') {
            const histDivFilter = document.getElementById('hist-div-filter');
            if (histDivFilter && histDivFilter.options.length <= 1) {
                fetch('/api/divisions')
                    .then(r => r.json())
                    .then(divs => { 
                        divs.forEach(d => { histDivFilter.innerHTML += `<option value="${d}">${d}</option>`; }); 
                    }).catch(e => console.error(e));
            }
        }
        
        if (['players', 'diagnostics', 'manual'].includes(tabId) && playerDirectoryData.length === 0) {
            window.loadPlayerDirectory();
        }
        
        if (tabId === 'players') window.loadChaosConfig();
        
        if (tabId === 'diagnostics') {
            fetch('/api/admin/get_rating_scales')
                .then(r => r.json())
                .then(d => {
                    document.getElementById('math-k-win').value = d.k_win; 
                    document.getElementById('math-k-loss').value = d.k_loss;
                }).catch(e => console.error(e));
        }
        
        if (tabId === 'audit') { 
            window.loadAuditLogs(); 
            window.loadAdminUsers(); 
            window.loadTrafficStats(); 
        }
    };

    window.forceRefresh = async function() {
        if (!confirm("Force the server to pull the latest data from the Master Google Sheet?")) return;
        const btn = document.querySelector('button[onclick="window.forceRefresh()"]');
        const originalHtml = btn ? btn.innerHTML : "FORCE REFRESH";
        
        if (btn) { 
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> SYNCING...'; 
            btn.disabled = true; 
        }
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/refresh', { method: 'POST' });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success || data.status === "Refreshed") {
                alert("✅ Sync triggered! Changes will appear momentarily."); 
            } else { 
                alert("❌ Sync failed."); 
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("❌ Network Error."); 
        } finally { 
            if (btn) { btn.innerHTML = originalHtml; btn.disabled = false; } 
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // PLAYER SEARCH UTILITIES
    // ==========================================
    let playerDirectoryData = [];
    
    window.loadPlayerDirectory = async function() { 
        try { 
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/player_directory'); 
            playerDirectoryData = await res.json(); 
        } catch(e) {
            console.error("Failed to load player directory", e);
        } 
    };

    function attachPlayerSearch(inputId, suggId, hiddenId = null) {
        const input = document.getElementById(inputId); 
        const sugg = document.getElementById(suggId);
        
        if (!input || !sugg) return;
        
        input.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const q = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
            if (q.length < 1) { 
                sugg.classList.add('hidden'); 
                return; 
            }
            
            const matches = playerDirectoryData.filter(p => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
            
            if (matches.length === 0) { 
                sugg.innerHTML = '<div class="px-3 py-2 text-slate-400 text-xs italic">No players found</div>'; 
                sugg.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                return; 
            }
            
            sugg.innerHTML = matches.slice(0, 8).map(p => {
                const ratingInfo = p.true_rating !== undefined
                    ? `Pub: ${p.rating} | True: ${p.true_rating} (±${p.rd || 35})`
                    : `${p.id}`;
                return `
                <div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-blue-600/30 cursor-pointer text-sm text-white font-bold border-b border-white/5 flex justify-between items-center" 
                     onclick="
                     document.getElementById('${inputId}').value = '${p.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}'; 
                     ${hiddenId ? `document.getElementById('${hiddenId}').value = '${p.id}';` : ''} 
                     document.getElementById('${suggId}').classList.add('hidden');
                     ${hiddenId === 'override-id' ? `window.fetchRatingContext('${p.id}');` : ''}
                     ">
                    <span>${p.name}</span>
                    <span class="text-[10px] text-purple-300 font-mono ml-2">${ratingInfo}</span>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
            
            sugg.classList.remove('hidden');
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { 
            if (e.target !== input && e.target !== sugg) {
                sugg.classList.add('hidden'); 
            }
        });
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        window.setAdminCategory('ops'); 
        attachPlayerSearch('override-search', 'override-suggestions', 'override-id');
        attachPlayerSearch('merge-bad-search', 'merge-bad-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('merge-good-search', 'merge-good-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('diag-p1-search', 'diag-p1-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('diag-p2-search', 'diag-p2-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('manual-p1', 'sugg-p1');
        attachPlayerSearch('manual-p2', 'sugg-p2');
        attachPlayerSearch('unlink-search', 'unlink-suggestions');
    });

    // ==========================================
    // ACCOUNT LINKING APPROVALS
    // ==========================================
    window.loadPendingAccounts = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('pendingAccountsList');
        if (!list) return;
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl text-indigo-500"></i></div>';

        if (playerDirectoryData.length === 0) await window.loadPlayerDirectory();

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/pending_accounts');
            const rawData = await res.json();
            const data = Array.isArray(rawData) ? rawData : (rawData.pending_users || []);

            if (data.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-xl">Awesome! No pending accounts to approve.</div>';
                return;
            }

            let html = '';
            data.forEach(a => {
                const uid = a.uid || a.id;
                html += `
                <div class="bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 shadow-lg mb-3">
                    <div>
                        <div class="font-bold text-white text-lg">${a.name}</div>
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">${a.email} • DOB: ${a.dob || 'N/A'}</div>
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono mt-1">UID: ${uid}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/5 w-full md:w-auto">
                        <div class="relative w-full md:w-64">
                            <input type="text" id="link-search-${uid}" placeholder="Link to official player..." class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-white text-xs outline-none focus:border-indigo-500" autocomplete="off" value="${a.name}">
                            <div id="link-sugg-${uid}" class="absolute z-50 left-0 right-0 top-full mt-1 bg-slate-700 rounded shadow-xl hidden border border-white/10 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar"></div>
                        </div>
                        <button onclick="window.linkAccount('${uid}')" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-bold py-2.5 px-4 rounded-lg text-xs transition shadow-lg whitespace-nowrap"><i class="fa-solid fa-link mr-1"></i> Approve</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;

            data.forEach(a => {
                const uid = a.uid || a.id;
                attachPlayerSearch(`link-search-${uid}`, `link-sugg-${uid}`);
            });
        } catch(e) {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-400 text-center py-10">Error loading pending accounts.</div>';
        }
    };

    window.linkAccount = async function(uid) {
        const playerName = document.getElementById(`link-search-${uid}`).value;
        if (!playerName) return alert("Please select an official player name to link.");
        if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to officially link UID ${uid} to the Glicko profile for ${playerName}?`)) return;

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/approve_account', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: uid, player_name: playerName })
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("✅ Account linked and approved! The player can now access their stats in the App.");
                window.loadPendingAccounts();
            } else {
                alert("❌ Failed to link account.");
            }
        } catch(e) {
            alert("Network Error");
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // MANUAL MATCH & HISTORY
    // ==========================================
    window.submitManualMatch = async function() {
        const p1 = document.getElementById('manual-p1').value; 
        const p2 = document.getElementById('manual-p2').value;
        const date = document.getElementById('manual-date').value; 
        const scores = document.getElementById('manual-scores').value;
        
        if (!p1 || !p2 || !date) return alert("Player 1, Player 2, and Date are required.");
        if (!confirm(`Create official match record?\n\n${p1} vs ${p2}\nDate: ${date}\nScores: ${scores || 'None provided'}`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/manual_match', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ p1: p1, p2: p2, date: date, scores: scores }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert(`✅ Match injected successfully! The database is auto-syncing in the background.`); 
                document.getElementById('manual-p1').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('manual-p2').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('manual-scores').value = ''; 
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error injecting match.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network error."); 
        }
    };

    window.searchHistory = async function() {
        const q = document.getElementById('history-search').value.trim(); 
        const divFilterObj = document.getElementById('hist-div-filter');
        const sourceFilterObj = document.getElementById('hist-source-filter');
        
        const divFilter = divFilterObj ? divFilterObj.value : 'All'; 
        const sourceFilter = sourceFilterObj ? sourceFilterObj.value : 'All';
        
        const list = document.getElementById('historyList'); 
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-indigo-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/history?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&source=${encodeURIComponent(sourceFilter)}`); 
            let matches = await res.json();
            
            if (divFilter !== 'All') {
                matches = matches.filter(m => m.division.toLowerCase() === divFilter.toLowerCase());
            }
            
            if (matches.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic">No matches found.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            let html = '';
            matches.forEach(m => {
                let gameHistoryHtml = '';
                if (m.game_history) {
                    gameHistoryHtml = `<div class="mt-3 bg-black/30 p-2 rounded border border-white/5 text-center text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">Set Scores: ${m.game_history}</div>`;
                } else if (m.rich_stats && m.rich_stats.total_duration) {
                    gameHistoryHtml = `<div class="mt-3 bg-blue-900/20 p-2 rounded border border-blue-500/20 text-center text-xs text-blue-300"><i class="fa-solid fa-tablet-screen-button"></i> iPad Match • Duration: ${m.rich_stats.total_duration}</div>`;
                }

                const sourceBadge = m.source === 'Spreadsheet' 
                    ? `<span class="bg-green-900/30 text-green-400 border border-green-500/20 text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shadow-inner"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-excel mr-1"></i>Sheet</span>` 
                    : `<span class="bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/20 text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shadow-inner"><i class="fa-solid fa-tablet mr-1"></i>App</span>`;

                html += `
                <div class="bg-black/30 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 mb-3 flex flex-col hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors">
                    <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 w-full">
                        <div class="flex-1 w-full">
                            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                                <span class="bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono border border-white/10">ID: ${m.match_id || 'N/A'}</span>
                                <span class="bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/20 text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded uppercase font-bold tracking-widest">${m.division}</span>
                                ${sourceBadge}
                            </div>
                            <div class="text-lg font-black text-white">${m.p1} <span class="text-slate-500 mx-2 text-sm">vs</span> ${m.p2}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-900/80 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/10 w-full md:w-auto shrink-0">
                            <div class="flex flex-col w-14">
                                <span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1 text-center">Score 1</span>
                                <input type="number" id="s1-${m.id}" value="${m.s1}" class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-1.5 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none text-sm">
                            </div>
                            <span class="text-slate-600 font-black mt-3">-</span>
                            <div class="flex flex-col w-14">
                                <span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1 text-center">Score 2</span>
                                <input type="number" id="s2-${m.id}" value="${m.s2}" class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-1.5 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none text-sm">
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex flex-col w-28 ml-2">
                                <span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1 text-center">Date</span>
                                <input type="text" id="date-${m.id}" value="${m.date}" class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-1.5 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none text-sm">
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex flex-col gap-1 ml-2 mt-4">
                                <button onclick="window.adminSaveHistoryEdit('${m.p1.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${m.p2.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${m.date}', '${m.id}')" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center"><i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk"></i></button>
                                <button onclick="document.getElementById('data-${m.match_id}').classList.toggle('hidden')" class="bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center" title="View Match Data"><i class="fa-solid fa-chart-simple"></i></button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div id="data-${m.match_id}" class="hidden w-full mt-4 bg-black/40 border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-4 flex-col gap-3">
                        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                            <div class="bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-white/5 relative overflow-hidden">
                                <div class="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-1 ${m.p1_delta > 0 ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-red-500'}"></div>
                                <div class="text-sky-400 font-bold mb-1 border-b border-white/10 pb-1 pl-3">${m.p1}</div>
                                <div class="text-sm text-slate-300 font-mono mt-2 pl-3">Rating: ${Math.round(m.p1_before)} <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right text-slate-600 mx-1 text-[10px]"></i> ${Math.round(m.p1_after)} <span class="${m.p1_delta > 0 ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'} ml-1 text-xs">(${m.p1_delta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${Math.round(m.p1_delta)})</span></div>
                                <div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono mt-1 pl-3">RD: ±${Math.round(m.p1_rd_before)} <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right mx-1 text-[10px]"></i> ±${Math.round(m.p1_rd_after)}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-white/5 relative overflow-hidden">
                                <div class="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-1 ${m.p2_delta > 0 ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-red-500'}"></div>
                                <div class="text-red-400 font-bold mb-1 border-b border-white/10 pb-1 pl-3">${m.p2}</div>
                                <div class="text-sm text-slate-300 font-mono mt-2 pl-3">Rating: ${Math.round(m.p2_before)} <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right text-slate-600 mx-1 text-[10px]"></i> ${Math.round(m.p2_after)} <span class="${m.p2_delta > 0 ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'} ml-1 text-xs">(${m.p2_delta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${Math.round(m.p2_delta)})</span></div>
                                <div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono mt-1 pl-3">RD: ±${Math.round(m.p2_rd_before)} <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right mx-1 text-[10px]"></i> ±${Math.round(m.p2_rd_after)}</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        ${gameHistoryHtml}
                        
                        <div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-white/10 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
                            <div class="text-[10px] text-yellow-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest"><i class="fa-solid fa-wrench mr-1"></i> Override Point Changes</div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
