                                <input type="number" step="0.1" id="override-p1-${m.match_id}" placeholder="${m.p1} pts" class="w-24 bg-black/40 border border-white/10 text-white text-center rounded py-1.5 text-xs focus:ring-1 focus:ring-yellow-500 outline-none" value="${m.p1_delta ? Math.round(m.p1_delta) : ''}">
                                <input type="number" step="0.1" id="override-p2-${m.match_id}" placeholder="${m.p2} pts" class="w-24 bg-black/40 border border-white/10 text-white text-center rounded py-1.5 text-xs focus:ring-1 focus:ring-yellow-500 outline-none" value="${m.p2_delta ? Math.round(m.p2_delta) : ''}">
                                <button onclick="window.saveDeltaOverride('${m.match_id}', '${m.p1.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${m.p2.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-500 text-black font-black px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs transition shadow-lg">SAVE</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) { 
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-400 text-center py-10">Error loading history.</div>'; 
        }
    };

    window.saveDeltaOverride = async function(matchId, p1, p2) {
        const p1Delta = document.getElementById(`override-p1-${matchId}`).value; 
        const p2Delta = document.getElementById(`override-p2-${matchId}`).value;
        
        if (!p1Delta || !p2Delta) return alert("Please enter point changes for both players.");
        if (!confirm(`OVERRIDE MATH:\n\nForce ${p1} to get ${p1Delta} pts\nForce ${p2} to get ${p2Delta} pts\n\nProceed?`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/override_deltas', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ match_id: matchId, p1_delta: p1Delta, p2_delta: p2Delta }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert("✅ Point overrides saved!"); 
                window.searchHistory(); 
            } else { 
                alert("❌ Failed to save."); 
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    window.adminSaveHistoryEdit = async function(p1, p2, oldDate, id) {
        const s1 = document.getElementById(`s1-${id}`).value; 
        const s2 = document.getElementById(`s2-${id}`).value; 
        const newDate = document.getElementById(`date-${id}`).value;
        
        if (!confirm(`OVERRIDE MATCH:\n\nPlayers: ${p1} vs ${p2}\nNew Score: ${s1}-${s2}\nNew Date: ${newDate}\n\nProceed?`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/update_match', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ p1: p1, p2: p2, date: oldDate, s1: s1, s2: s2, new_date: newDate }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("✅ Update locked in!"); 
            } else {
                alert("❌ Failed to update match.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // MISSING DATE FIXER
    // ==========================================
    window.loadDateErrors = async function() {
        const dList = document.getElementById('dateErrorsList'); 
        const dBadge = document.getElementById('date-badge');
        if (!dList) return; 
        
        dList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-orange-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/date_errors'); 
            const errors = await res.json();
            
            if (dBadge) { 
                dBadge.innerText = errors.length; 
                errors.length > 0 ? dBadge.classList.remove('hidden') : dBadge.classList.add('hidden'); 
            }
            
            if (errors.length === 0) { 
                dList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-600 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-xl">Awesome! No missing dates found.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            let html = "";
            errors.forEach(m => {
                html += `
                <div id="date-card-${m.id}" class="date-card bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 shadow-lg mb-3" data-season="${m.season}" data-div="${m.division}" data-week="${m.week}">
                    <div class="flex-1 w-full">
                        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center mb-2">
                            <span class="bg-red-900/30 text-red-400 border border-red-500/20 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded">NO DATE</span>
                            <span class="bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/20 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded shadow-inner"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-excel mr-1"></i>${m.sheet_name} • ROW ${m.row_index}</span>
                            <span class="bg-yellow-900/30 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/20 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded shadow-inner uppercase">${m.division} • WK ${m.week}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="font-black text-white text-lg">${m.home_players[0]} <span class="text-slate-500 mx-2 text-sm">vs</span> ${m.away_players[0]}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/5 shrink-0">
                        <div class="flex flex-col">
                            <span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Fix Date (DD/MM/YYYY)</span>
                            <input type="text" id="fix-date-${m.id}" placeholder="e.g. 14/02/2026" class="w-28 bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-1 focus:border-orange-500 focus:outline-none text-xs">
                        </div>
                        <button onclick="window.adminBulkDateFix('${m.season}', '${m.division}', '${m.week}', '${m.id}', this)" class="bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-500 text-white font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded text-xs transition-transform hover:scale-105 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(249,115,22,0.4)] mt-3">FIX</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            dList.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) { 
            dList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-400 text-center py-10">Error loading date errors.</div>'; 
        }
    };

    window.adminBulkDateFix = async function(season, division, week, id, btnElement) {
        const newDate = document.getElementById(`fix-date-${id}`).value;
        if (!newDate) return alert("Please enter a valid date.");
        
        btnElement.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>'; 
        btnElement.disabled = true;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/bulk_fix_date', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ season: season, division: division, week: week, date: newDate }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if (data.success) {
                const allCards = document.querySelectorAll('.date-card'); 
                let removedCount = 0;
                allCards.forEach(card => {
                    if (card.dataset.season === season && card.dataset.div === division && card.dataset.week === week) {
                        card.style.transition = "all 0.3s ease"; 
                        card.style.opacity = "0"; 
                        card.style.transform = "translateX(20px)"; 
                        setTimeout(() => card.remove(), 300); 
                        removedCount++;
                    }
                });
                
                const dBadge = document.getElementById('date-badge');
                if (dBadge) { 
                    let count = parseInt(dBadge.innerText) || 0; 
                    count = Math.max(0, count - removedCount); 
                    dBadge.innerText = count; 
                    if (count === 0) { 
                        dBadge.classList.add('hidden'); 
                        document.getElementById('dateErrorsList').innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-600 text-center py-10 italic">Awesome! No missing dates found.</div>'; 
                    } 
                }
            } else { 
                alert("❌ Failed to set bulk date fix."); 
                btnElement.innerHTML = 'FIX'; 
                btnElement.disabled = false; 
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
            btnElement.innerHTML = 'FIX'; 
            btnElement.disabled = false; 
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // PDF PARSER & TEAM ROSTER EDITOR
    // ==========================================
    window.uploadPdfSchedule = async function() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdf-file-upload'); 
        const seasonInput = document.getElementById('pdf-season'); 
        const divInput = document.getElementById('pdf-division'); 
        const btn = document.getElementById('pdf-upload-btn');
        
        if (!fileInput.files.length) return alert("Please select a PDF file first.");
        if (!seasonInput.value) return alert("Please specify the Season (e.g., Summer 2026).");
        if (!divInput.value) return alert("Please specify the Division.");
        
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('pdf', fileInput.files[0]); 
        formData.append('season', seasonInput.value);
        formData.append('division', divInput.value);
        
        const originalText = btn.innerHTML; 
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> PARSING PDF & EXTRACTING TEAMS...'; 
        btn.disabled = true;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/upload_schedule', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert(`✅ Success! AI extracted ${data.teams_found} teams and ${data.matches_found} matchups from the PDF.`); 
                fileInput.value = ''; 
                divInput.value = ''; 
                seasonInput.value = '';
                
                if (document.getElementById('admin-team-editor') && !document.getElementById('admin-team-editor').classList.contains('hidden')) {
                    window.loadTeams();
                }
            } else {
                alert(`❌ Parsing Error: ${data.error}`);
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        } finally { 
            btn.innerHTML = originalText; 
            btn.disabled = false; 
        }
    };

    window.loadTeams = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('teamsList');
        if (!list) return;
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-10"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl text-emerald-500"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/teams'); 
            const teams = await res.json();
            
            if (teams.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center text-slate-500 py-10 italic">No teams found. Upload a schedule PDF first to auto-extract teams!</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            let html = '';
            teams.forEach(t => {
                const pInputs = [0, 1, 2].map(i => {
                    const pName = t.players && t.players[i] ? t.players[i] : '';
                    return `<input type="text" id="team-${t.id}-p${i}" value="${pName.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" placeholder="Player ${i+1}" class="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white mb-2 focus:border-emerald-500 outline-none">`;
                }).join('');
                
                html += `
                <div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-white/5 shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div class="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-1 bg-emerald-500"></div>
                    <div class="pl-3">
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">${t.season || 'Unknown Season'} • ${t.division || 'Unknown Division'}</div>
                        <h3 class="text-white font-black text-lg mb-4 pb-2 border-b border-white/10">${t.team_name || t.id}</h3>
                        <div class="space-y-1 mb-4">${pInputs}</div>
                        <button onclick="window.saveTeam('${t.id}')" class="w-full bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm transition shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(16,185,129,0.3)]">Save Roster</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) { 
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-red-500 text-center py-10">Error loading teams.</div>'; 
        }
    };
    
    window.saveTeam = async function(teamId) {
        const players = [];
        for(let i=0; i<3; i++) { 
            const val = document.getElementById(`team-${teamId}-p${i}`).value.trim(); 
            if (val) players.push(val); 
        }
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/update_team', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ team_id: teamId, players: players }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert("✅ Team saved successfully!"); 
            } else { 
                alert("❌ Error saving team."); 
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // PLAYER EDITOR & RATING OVERRIDES
    // ==========================================
    window.fetchRatingContext = async function(playerId) {
        const contextDiv = document.getElementById('override-context');
        if (!contextDiv) return;
        
        contextDiv.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Loading recent rating context...';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/rating_context/${playerId}`);
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if (data.length === 0) {
                contextDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="italic">No recent match history found for this player.</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            let html = '<div class="font-bold text-white mb-2 pb-1 border-b border-white/10"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left mr-2"></i>Last 6 Match Ratings:</div>';
            data.forEach(m => {
                const trueTag = m.true_rating !== undefined ? ` <span class="text-purple-400">(T: ${m.true_rating})</span>` : '';
                html += `<div class="flex justify-between items-center py-1 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0"><span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest">${m.date} vs ${m.opponent}</span> <span class="text-emerald-400 font-mono font-bold">${m.rating}${trueTag}</span></div>`;
            });
            
            contextDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="bg-black/30 p-3 rounded border border-white/5 shadow-inner">${html}</div>`;
        } catch(e) {
            contextDiv.innerHTML = '';
        }
    };

    window.submitRatingOverride = async function() {
        const id = document.getElementById('override-id').value; 
        const rating = document.getElementById('override-score').value; 
        const sd = document.getElementById('override-sd').value;
        const isRetro = document.getElementById('override-retro') ? document.getElementById('override-retro').checked : true;
        const effectiveDate = document.getElementById('override-effective-date') ? document.getElementById('override-effective-date').value : '';
        
        if (!id || !rating) return alert("Please select a player and provide a rating.");
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/override_rating', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    player_id: id,
                    rating: rating,
                    sd: sd,
                    retroactive: isRetro,
                    effective_date: effectiveDate
                }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if (data.success) { 
                alert(`✅ Successfully applied rating checkpoint override!`); 
                document.getElementById('override-id').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('override-search').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('override-score').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('override-sd').value = '';
                if (document.getElementById('override-effective-date')) document.getElementById('override-effective-date').value = '';
                if (document.getElementById('override-context')) document.getElementById('override-context').innerHTML = '';
                window.loadPlayerDirectory();
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error applying rating override.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network error."); 
        }
    };
    
    window.bulkSetRatings = async function() {
        if (!confirm("⚠️ Are you sure you want to pull ALL ratings from the 'Ratings crap' sheet and apply them as of today?")) return;
        const btn = document.querySelector('button[onclick="window.bulkSetRatings()"]'); 
        const originalHtml = btn ? btn.innerHTML : "SET RATINGS";
        
        if (btn) { 
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> PULLING RATINGS...'; 
            btn.disabled = true; 
        }
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/bulk_pull_ratings', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({}) 
            });
            const text = await res.text(); 
            let data;
            try { 
                data = JSON.parse(text); 
            } catch(err) { 
                alert("Server Crash!"); 
                return; 
            }
            if (data.success) { 
                alert(`✅ Successfully imported ${data.count} ratings!`); 
                window.loadPlayerDirectory(); 
            } else {
                alert(`❌ Failed: ${data.error}`);
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network error: " + e.message); 
        } finally { 
            if (btn) { btn.innerHTML = originalHtml; btn.disabled = false; } 
        }
    };

    window.submitPlayerMerge = async function() {
        const bad = document.getElementById('merge-bad-search').value; 
        const good = document.getElementById('merge-good-search').value;
        
        if (!bad || !good) return alert("Please select both names.");
        if (bad.toLowerCase() === good.toLowerCase()) return alert("Names must be different.");
        if (!confirm(`⚠️ DANGER: You are about to PERMANENTLY merge "${bad}" into "${good}". Proceed?`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/merge', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ bad_name: bad, good_name: good }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert(`✅ Successfully merged profiles!`); 
                document.getElementById('merge-bad-search').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('merge-good-search').value = ''; 
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error merging players.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network error."); 
        }
    };

    window.unlinkPlayerAccount = async function() {
        const pName = document.getElementById('unlink-search').value;
        if (!pName) return alert("Please select a player to unlink.");
        
        if (!confirm(`⚠️ WARNING ⚠️\n\nAre you sure you want to delete the app login account for ${pName}?\n\nThis will NOT delete their matches or stats. It just allows them to link a new email and sign up again.`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/unlink_account', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ player_name: pName })
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if (data.success) {
                alert(`✅ Successfully deleted the login account for ${pName}. They can now sign up again!`);
                document.getElementById('unlink-search').value = '';
                if (document.getElementById('admin-audit')) window.loadAuditLogs(); 
            } else {
                alert("❌ Failed to unlink account.");
            }
        } catch(e) { alert("Network Error"); }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // SYSTEM MATH & DIAGNOSTICS
    // ==========================================
    window.saveMathMultipliers = async function() {
        const kWin = document.getElementById('math-k-win').value;
        const kLoss = document.getElementById('math-k-loss').value;
        if (!confirm(`WARNING: Changing these scales triggers a recalculation of the entire database.\n\nNew Win Scale: ${kWin}x\nNew Loss Scale: ${kLoss}x\n\nProceed?`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/set_rating_scales', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ k_win: kWin, k_loss: kLoss }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert("✅ Math parameters updated! The server is recalculating history."); 
                window.location.reload(); 
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error saving parameters.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    window.runDiagnostics = async function() {
        const p1 = document.getElementById('diag-p1-search').value; 
        const p2 = document.getElementById('diag-p2-search').value; 
        const s1 = document.getElementById('diag-s1').value; 
        const s2 = document.getElementById('diag-s2').value;
        const resultsBox = document.getElementById('diag-results'); 
        const btn = document.getElementById('diag-btn');
        
        if (!p1 || !p2) return alert("Please select both players.");
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Calculating...';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/glicko_calc', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ p1: p1, p2: p2, s1: s1, s2: s2 }) 
            });
            const d = await res.json();
            if (d.success) {
                const math = d.data;
                const renderPlayerMath = (pData) => {
                    const deltaColor = pData.delta > 0 ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-red-400";
                    const deltaSign = pData.delta > 0 ? "+" : "";
                    return `
                    <div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-white/5 relative">
                        <div class="font-black text-white text-lg mb-4 border-b border-white/10 pb-2">${pData.name}</div>
                        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                            <div>
                                <div class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 mb-1">Current Rating</div>
                                <div class="text-xl font-mono text-white">${pData.old_rating} <span class="text-xs text-slate-500">(±${pData.old_rd})</span></div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <div class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 mb-1">Expected Win %</div>
                                <div class="text-xl font-mono text-sky-400">${pData.expected_win_pct}%</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-white/10 flex justify-between items-center bg-black/20 p-3 rounded-lg">
                            <div class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500">Calculated Shift</div>
                            <div class="text-2xl font-black font-mono ${deltaColor}">${deltaSign}${pData.delta} pts</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-center mt-3">
                            <span class="text-[10px] uppercase text-slate-500 font-bold">Resulting Rating: </span>
                            <span class="text-white font-mono font-bold">${pData.new_rating}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                };
                resultsBox.innerHTML = `<div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${renderPlayerMath(math.p1)}${renderPlayerMath(math.p2)}</div>`;
                resultsBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        } finally { 
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-microchip"></i> Run Simulation'; 
        }
    };

    window.loadChaosConfig = async function() {
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/chaos_config'); 
            const data = await res.json();
            
            const req = data.req || 3; 
            const current = data.approvals ? data.approvals.length : 0; 
            const pct = Math.min(100, (current / req) * 100);
            
            const countEl = document.getElementById('chaos-vote-count'); 
            if (countEl) countEl.innerText = `${current}/${req}`;
            
            const prog = document.getElementById('chaos-progress-bar'); 
            if (prog) prog.style.width = `${pct}%`;
            
            const votersList = document.getElementById('chaos-voters-list'); 
            if (votersList) { 
                if (data.approvals && data.approvals.length > 0) votersList.innerText = `Voters: ${data.approvals.join(', ')}`; 
                else votersList.innerText = "Waiting for signatures..."; 
            }
            
            const weeksInput = document.getElementById('chaos-weeks'); 
            if (weeksInput && data.weeks && data.weeks.length > 0) weeksInput.value = data.weeks.join(', ');
            
            const banner = document.getElementById('chaos-status-banner');
            if (banner) {
                if (data.active) {
                    banner.className = "bg-red-900/30 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 animate-pulse";
                    banner.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-fire"></i> CHAOS MODE ARMED & ACTIVE';
                    document.getElementById('chaos-progress-bar').classList.remove('bg-red-500'); 
                    document.getElementById('chaos-progress-bar').classList.add('bg-red-500', 'shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(239,68,68,0.8)]');
                } else {
                    banner.className = "bg-green-900/30 border border-green-500/30 text-green-400 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 mb-4";
                    banner.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-lock"></i> Anti-Riot is ON (Default Safe Mode)';
                }
            }
        } catch(e) { }
    };
    
    window.castChaosVote = async function() {
        const weeksRaw = document.getElementById('chaos-weeks').value;
        if (!weeksRaw) return alert("You must specify which weeks to apply Chaos Mode to.");
        const weeks = weeksRaw.split(',').map(w => w.trim()).filter(w => w.length > 0);
        if (!confirm(`Authorize Chaos Mode for: ${weeks.join(', ')}?`)) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/chaos_vote', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ weeks: weeks }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) window.loadChaosConfig(); 
            else alert("Failed to record signature.");
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };
    
    window.clearChaosVotes = async function() {
        if (!confirm("Revoke all approvals and restore Anti-Riot lock?")) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/chaos_clear', { method: 'POST' });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                document.getElementById('chaos-weeks').value = ''; 
                window.loadChaosConfig(); 
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // COMMUNITY FORUM MODERATION
    // ==========================================
    window.loadCommunityMod = async function() {
        const feed = document.getElementById('mod-forum-feed');
        if (!feed) return;
        feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-red-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/hub/forum');
            const posts = await res.json();
            
            if (posts.length === 0) { 
                feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 py-10 italic">No posts in the community yet.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            feed.innerHTML = posts.map(p => `
                <div class="bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex justify-between items-start gap-4">
                    <div class="flex-1">
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mb-1 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest"><span class="text-white">${p.author_name}</span> • ${p.time_str}</div>
                        <div class="text-sm text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap">${p.content}</div>
                        ${p.image_url ? `<img src="${p.image_url}" class="mt-3 h-24 rounded object-cover shadow-lg border border-white/10">` : ''}
                    </div>
                    <button onclick="window.deleteCommunityPost('${p.id}')" class="bg-red-900/40 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-500 hover:text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg transition shrink-0 border border-red-500/20"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        } catch(e) { 
            feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-500 py-10 text-center">Error loading feed.</div>'; 
        }
    };

    window.deleteCommunityPost = async function(id) {
        if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to permanently delete this post?")) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/delete_post', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'}, 
                body: JSON.stringify({post_id: id}) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert("Post deleted."); 
                window.loadCommunityMod(); 
            } else { 
                alert("Failed to delete post."); 
            }
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    // ==========================================
    // NOTICES, SMS INBOX, AND FEEDBACK
    // ==========================================
    window.loadPendingReports = async function() {
        const fList = document.getElementById('feedbackList'); 
        if (!fList) return; 
        fList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-slate-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/reports'); 
            const reports = await res.json(); 
            let fHtml = ""; 
            
            reports.forEach(r => {
                if (r.type === 'FEEDBACK') {
                    fHtml += `
                    <div class="bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-500/20 shadow-lg mb-3">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                            <div>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-blue-400 bg-blue-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-blue-500/20 mr-2">${r.title}</span>
                                <span class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">${r.date}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">From: <span class="text-slate-300 font-bold">${r.reporter}</span></div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="bg-black/30 border border-white/5 rounded p-3 mb-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm text-slate-300">${r.problem}</div>
                        <div class="flex gap-2 border-t border-white/5 pt-3">
                            <button onclick="window.resolveReport('${r.id}', 'approve')" class="flex-1 bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded text-sm transition-colors shadow-lg"><i class="fa-solid fa-check mr-1"></i> Mark Resolved</button>
                            <button onclick="window.resolveReport('${r.id}', 'reject')" class="flex-1 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded text-sm transition-colors shadow-lg"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i> Dismiss</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                }
            });
            fList.innerHTML = fHtml || '<div class="text-slate-600 text-center py-10 italic">No new feedback.</div>';
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.resolveReport = async function(id, action) {
        if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to ${action} this?`)) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/resolve', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ report_id: id, action: action }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { window.loadPendingReports(); } 
            else alert("Error resolving item.");
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network error."); 
        }
    };

    window.loadAdminNotices = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('adminNoticesList'); 
        if (!list) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/notices'); 
            const notices = await res.json();
            if (notices.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-600 text-center py-10 italic">No active notices.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            list.innerHTML = notices.map(n => {
                let badgeColor = 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300';
                if (n.type === 'Update') badgeColor = 'bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400 border border-sky-500/30';
                if (n.type === 'Event') badgeColor = 'bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/30';
                if (n.type === 'Alert') badgeColor = 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border border-red-500/30';
                return `
                <div class="bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex justify-between items-start gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded uppercase tracking-widest ${badgeColor}">${n.type}</span>
                            <span class="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-mono">${n.date_str}</span>
                        </div>
                        <h4 class="text-white font-bold text-lg leading-tight mb-2">${n.title}</h4>
                        <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 line-clamp-2">${n.message}</p>
                    </div>
                    <button onclick="window.deleteNotice('${n.id}')" class="bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-500 hover:text-white border border-red-500/20 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors shrink-0"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.submitNotice = async function() {
        const title = document.getElementById('notice-title').value; 
        const type = document.getElementById('notice-type').value; 
        const msg = document.getElementById('notice-message').value;
        if (!title || !msg) return alert("Title and Message are required.");
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/add_notice', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ title: title, type: type, message: msg }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                document.getElementById('notice-title').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('notice-message').value = ''; 
                window.loadAdminNotices(); 
                alert("✅ Notice posted to public site!"); 
            } else {
                alert("❌ Failed to post notice.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    window.deleteNotice = async function(id) {
        if (!confirm("Remove this notice from the public site?")) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/delete_notice', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ notice_id: id }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) window.loadAdminNotices(); 
            else alert("Failed to delete notice.");
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.loadSmsInbox = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('smsInboxList'); 
        if (!list) return;
        
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-pink-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/sms_inbox'); 
            const messages = await res.json();
            
            if (messages.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-8 italic border border-white/5 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-xl">No replies received yet.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            list.innerHTML = messages.map(m => `
                <div class="bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-pink-500/20 shadow">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-2 border-b border-white/5 pb-2">
                        <span class="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-pink-400"><i class="fa-solid fa-mobile-screen-button mr-2"></i>${m.from}</span>
                        <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">${m.time_str}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-sm text-slate-200 font-bold">${m.body}</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        } catch(e) { 
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-500 text-center py-8 italic">Failed to load SMS inbox.</div>'; 
        }
    };

    window.loadAdminChat = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('adminChatList'); 
        if (!list) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/messages'); 
            const messages = await res.json();
            if (messages.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-8 italic">No notes yet.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            list.innerHTML = messages.map(m => `
                <div class="bg-slate-900/50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-white/5 relative">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-2 border-b border-white/5 pb-2">
                        <span class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-400"><i class="fa-solid fa-user-shield mr-1"></i>${m.author}</span>
                        <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">${m.time_str}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-sm text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap">${m.message}</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.postAdminMessage = async function() {
        const input = document.getElementById('admin-chat-input'); 
        const msg = input.value.trim(); 
        if (!msg) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/add_message', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                body: JSON.stringify({ message: msg }) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                input.value = ''; 
                window.loadAdminChat(); 
            } else {
                alert("Failed to post message.");
            }
        } catch(e) { 
            alert("Network Error"); 
        }
    };

    // ==========================================
    // SYSTEM AUDIT & DONATIONS
    // ==========================================
    window.loadDonations = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('donationsList'); 
        const totalEl = document.getElementById('donations-total'); 
        const countEl = document.getElementById('donations-count');
        if (!list) return;
        
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-emerald-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/donations'); 
            const data = await res.json();
            let total = 0; 
            let html = '';
            
            if (data.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic">No donations recorded yet.</div>'; 
                if (totalEl) totalEl.innerText = '$0.00'; 
                if (countEl) countEl.innerText = '0'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            data.forEach(d => {
                total += d.amount;
                html += `
                <div class="bg-black/30 p-4 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/20 flex justify-between items-center transition-colors hover:bg-slate-800">
                    <div>
                        <div class="font-bold text-white text-lg">${d.name}</div>
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono tracking-widest mt-1">${d.time_str}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-400 font-mono bg-emerald-900/20 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/30">$${d.amount.toFixed(2)}</div>
                </div>`;
            });
            
            list.innerHTML = html;
            if (totalEl) totalEl.innerText = '$' + total.toFixed(2); 
            if (countEl) countEl.innerText = data.length;
        } catch (e) { 
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-400 text-center py-10">Error loading donations.</div>'; 
        }
    };

    window.loadTrafficStats = async function() {
        const viewsEl = document.getElementById('dash-traffic-views'); 
        const uniquesEl = document.getElementById('dash-traffic-uniques');
        if (!viewsEl || !uniquesEl) return;
        try { 
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/traffic'); 
            const data = await res.json(); 
            viewsEl.innerText = data.views; 
            uniquesEl.innerText = data.uniques; 
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.loadAuditLogs = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('auditLogList'); 
        if (!list) return;
        const filterObj = document.getElementById('audit-filter');
        const filter = filterObj ? filterObj.value : 'ALL';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/audit_logs'); 
            const logs = await res.json();
            
            if (logs.length === 0) { 
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 py-10 text-center italic">No actions recorded yet.</div>'; 
                return; 
            }
            
            let html = '';
            logs.forEach(l => {
                if (filter === 'MATCH' && !['UPDATE_MATCH', 'ADD_MATCH', 'FORCE_FINISH_LIVE', 'OVERRIDE_DELTAS'].includes(l.action)) return;
                if (filter === 'RATING' && !['OVERRIDE_RATING', 'UPDATE_MATH', 'BULK_IMPORT_RATINGS'].includes(l.action)) return;
                if (filter === 'SYSTEM' && !['SESSION_START', 'SESSION_END', 'FORCE_REFRESH'].includes(l.action)) return;

                const isUndone = l.status === 'undone'; 
                const bgClass = isUndone ? 'bg-slate-800/30 border-dashed opacity-50' : 'bg-slate-800';
                const noUndoActions = ['MERGE_PLAYER', 'ADD_NOTICE', 'DELETE_NOTICE', 'SET_FORMAT', 'FORCE_REFRESH', 'RESOLVE_INBOX', 'SMS_BROADCAST', 'SESSION_START', 'SESSION_END', 'APPROVE_ACCOUNT', 'DELETE_ACCOUNT'];
                const undoBtn = (!isUndone && !noUndoActions.includes(l.action)) 
                    ? `<button onclick="window.undoAction('${l.id}')" class="text-[10px] bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded transition shadow">UNDO</button>` 
                    : '';
                
                html += `
                <div class="${bgClass} border border-white/5 p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center gap-3 mb-3 shadow-lg">
                    <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 w-full">
                        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                            <span class="text-[9px] bg-slate-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded uppercase font-bold tracking-widest shadow-inner">${l.action}</span>
                            <span class="text-[11px] text-sky-400 font-mono font-bold bg-sky-900/20 px-2.5 py-1 rounded border border-sky-500/20 shadow-inner flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <i class="fa-regular fa-clock"></i> ${l.time_str}
                            </span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-sm font-bold text-white leading-snug">${l.description}</div>
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Authorized by: <span class="text-slate-300 font-bold ml-1">${l.admin}</span></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex flex-col gap-2 shrink-0 md:items-end">
                        ${undoBtn}
                        ${isUndone ? `<div class="text-[9px] text-red-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest border border-red-500/30 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-red-900/20 text-center">REVERSED BY ${l.undone_by}</div>` : ''}
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html || '<div class="text-slate-500 py-10 text-center italic">No logs match this filter.</div>';
        } catch(e) {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-400 py-10 text-center italic">Failed to load logs.</div>';
        }
    };

    window.undoAction = async function(logId) {
        if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to completely reverse this action?")) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/undo_action', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, 
                body: JSON.stringify({log_id: logId}) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert("✅ Action successfully undone!"); 
                window.loadAuditLogs(); 
            } else { 
                alert("❌ Failed to undo action."); 
            }
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.loadAdminUsers = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('adminUsersList'); 
        if (!list) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/users'); 
            const users = await res.json(); 
            let html = '';
            
            users.forEach(u => {
                const isPending = u.role === 'pending'; 
                const isTemp = u.role === 'temp_super_admin';
                let roleBadge = '';
                
                if (isPending) {
                    roleBadge = '<span class="text-yellow-500 bg-yellow-900/30 border border-yellow-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] uppercase font-bold">Pending</span>';
                } else if (isTemp) {
                    roleBadge = `<span class="text-pink-400 bg-pink-900/30 border border-pink-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] uppercase font-bold">Temp Super Admin <i class="fa-solid fa-clock text-[8px] ml-1"></i> ${u.expires_at_str}</span>`;
                } else {
                    roleBadge = `<span class="text-blue-400 bg-blue-900/30 border border-blue-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] uppercase font-bold">${u.role}</span>`;
                }
                
                let actions = '';
                if (u.role !== 'super_admin') {
                    if (isPending) { 
                        actions = `
                        <button onclick="window.manageUser('${u.email}', 'approve')" class="text-green-500 hover:text-green-400 text-sm mx-1"><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i></button>
                        <button onclick="window.manageUser('${u.email}', 'delete')" class="text-red-500 hover:text-red-400 text-sm mx-1"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>`; 
                    } else { 
                        actions = `<button onclick="window.manageUser('${u.email}', 'revoke')" class="text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase border border-red-500/30 px-2 py-1 rounded">Revoke Access</button>`; 
                        if (u.role === 'admin') {
                            actions += `<button onclick="window.manageUser('${u.email}', 'temp_super')" class="ml-2 text-pink-400 hover:text-pink-300 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase border border-pink-500/30 px-2 py-1 rounded">+ 7 Day Super Admin</button>`;
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                html += `
                <div class="bg-black/30 border border-white/5 p-3 rounded-lg flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <div class="text-sm font-bold text-white mb-1 truncate" title="${u.email}">${u.email}</div>
                        ${roleBadge}
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">${actions}</div>
                </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.manageUser = async function(email, action) {
        if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to apply this access rule for ${email}?`)) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/approve_user', { 
                method: 'POST', 
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, 
                body: JSON.stringify({email: email, action: action}) 
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) window.loadAdminUsers();
        } catch(e) {}
    };
</script>

<script type="module">
    import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.8.0/firebase-app.js";
    import { getFirestore, collection, query, onSnapshot } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.8.0/firebase-firestore.js";

    const firebaseConfig = { 
        apiKey: "AIzaSyDq05wFsKLHtt8G0Z5yLOG9pIk9UX24S4o", 
        authDomain: "gctta-stats-live.firebaseapp.com", 
        projectId: "gctta-stats-live", 
        storageBucket: "gctta-stats-live.firebasestorage.app", 
        messagingSenderId: "1070856258556", 
        appId: "1:1070856258556:web:be438e04c1941abc3c6879" 
    };
    
    try {
        const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
        const db = getFirestore(app);

        window.setFormatOverride = async function(fixtureId, formatType) {
            try { 
                await fetch('/api/admin/set_fixture_format', { 
                    method: 'POST', 
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                    body: JSON.stringify({ fixture_id: fixtureId, format_type: formatType }) 
                }); 
            } catch(e) {}
        };

        onSnapshot(query(collection(db, "fixture_schedule")), (snapshot) => {
            const liveList = document.getElementById("live-list"); 
            const scheduledList = document.getElementById("scheduled-list");
            let liveHtml = ""; 
            let schedHtml = "";
            
            snapshot.forEach((doc) => {
                const m = doc.data(); 
                const id = doc.id;
                const p1 = (m.home_players && m.home_players.length > 0) ? m.home_players[0] : m.home_team;
                const p2 = (m.away_players && m.away_players.length > 0) ? m.away_players[0] : m.away_team;
                
                if (m.match_status === "Live" || m.match_status === "Paused" || m.live_home_score > 0 || m.live_away_score > 0) {
                    const s1 = m.live_home_sets || 0; 
                    const s2 = m.live_away_sets || 0;
                    
                    liveHtml += `
                    <div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-blue-500/30 shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden mb-4">
                        <div class="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-blue-500 text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-bl-lg animate-pulse">ACTIVE NOW</div>
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 mt-1">
                            <span class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">Table ${m.table} • ${m.division}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-xl font-black text-white mb-4">${p1} <span class="text-slate-500 mx-2 text-sm">vs</span> ${p2}</div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-4 bg-black/40 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 mb-4 shadow-inner">
                            <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">Adjust Final Score:</div>
                            <input type="number" id="live-s1-${id}" value="${s1}" class="w-14 bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-2 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg">
                            <span class="text-slate-500 font-black">-</span>
                            <input type="number" id="live-s2-${id}" value="${s2}" class="w-14 bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-2 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg">
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex gap-3">
                            <button onclick="window.forceFinishLive('${id}', '${p1.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${p2.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02] shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(37,99,235,0.4)] flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-flag-checkered"></i> Force Finish</button>
                            <button onclick="window.wipeLiveMatch('${id}')" class="bg-red-900/50 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-400 hover:text-white border border-red-500/30 font-bold py-2.5 px-4 rounded-lg text-sm transition-colors" title="Wipe Error"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                } else if (m.match_status === "Scheduled" || !m.match_status) {
                    const formatVal = m.format_override || "Auto";
                    schedHtml += `
                    <div class="bg-slate-800/80 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 shadow relative">
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 border-b border-white/5 pb-1 flex justify-between">
                            <span>Table ${m.table}</span><span class="text-blue-400">${m.division}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="font-bold text-white text-sm mt-2">${m.home_team} <br><span class="text-slate-500 text-xs font-normal">vs</span><br> ${m.away_team}</div>
                        <div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-white/5">
                            <label class="block text-[9px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Match Format</label>
                            <select onchange="window.setFormatOverride('${id}', this.value)" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-slate-300 font-bold text-xs rounded py-2 px-2 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none">
                                <option value="Auto" ${formatVal === 'Auto' ? 'selected' : ''}>Auto-Detect</option>
                                <option value="2v2" ${formatVal === '2v2' ? 'selected' : ''}>Force 2v2 (5 Matches)</option>
                                <option value="3v3" ${formatVal === '3v3' ? 'selected' : ''}>Force 3v3 (11 Matches)</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                }
            });
            
            if (liveList) liveList.innerHTML = liveHtml || '<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-2 text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/30">No matches currently active.</div>';
            if (scheduledList) scheduledList.innerHTML = schedHtml || '<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-3 text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/30">No scheduled matches pending.</div>';
        });

        window.forceFinishLive = async function(id, p1, p2) {
            const s1 = document.getElementById(`live-s1-${id}`).value; 
            const s2 = document.getElementById(`live-s2-${id}`).value;
            
            if (!confirm(`FORCE FINISH MATCH:\n\n${p1} vs ${p2}\nFinal Score: ${s1} - ${s2}\n\nProceed?`)) return;
            
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/force_finish_live', { 
                    method: 'POST', 
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                    body: JSON.stringify({ id: id, s1: s1, s2: s2 }) 
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) alert("✅ Match forced finished!"); 
                else alert("❌ Error finishing match.");
            } catch(e) { 
                alert("Network Error"); 
            }
        };

        window.wipeLiveMatch = async function(id) {
            if (!confirm(`WIPE MATCH:\n\nAre you sure you want to completely erase this match?`)) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/wipe_live', { 
                    method: 'POST', 
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
                    body: JSON.stringify({ id: id }) 
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.success) alert("🗑️ Match wiped successfully!"); 
                else alert("❌ Error wiping match.");
            } catch(e) { 
                alert("Network Error"); 
            }
        };
    } catch(err) {
        console.warn("Firebase Live Connection skipped/failed. Check rules or CORS (harmless locally).");
    }
</script>

<script>
    function searchRC() {
        const name = document.getElementById('rc-search-input').value;
        if (!name) return;
        
        document.getElementById('rc-search-status').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('rc-search-results').innerHTML = '';
        
        fetch('/api/admin/search_rc', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({name: name})
        }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
            document.getElementById('rc-search-status').classList.add('hidden');
            if (data.success && data.results.length > 0) {
                document.getElementById('rc-search-results').innerHTML = data.results.map(r => `
                    <div class="bg-slate-900/50 border border-slate-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div class="text-white font-bold text-lg">\${r.name}</div>
                            <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-3 mt-1">
                                <span class="bg-sky-900/30 text-sky-400 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-sky-800/30">ID: \${r.id}</span>
                                <span><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot mr-1"></i>\${r.location}</span>
                                <span><i class="fa-solid fa-building mr-1"></i>\${r.club}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                            <div class="text-right">
                                <div class="text-2xl font-bold text-sky-400">\${r.rating.split('±')[0]}</div>
                                <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">±\${r.rating.split('±')[1] || '?'}</div>
                            </div>
                            <button onclick="linkRCHandler('\${r.name}', '\${r.id}')" class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white rounded transition-colors text-xs font-bold border border-slate-600">
                                Link Profile
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
            } else {
                document.getElementById('rc-search-results').innerHTML = `<div class="text-center text-slate-400 py-6">No players found.</div>`;
            }
        });
    }

    function linkRCHandler(rcName, rcId) {
        const localName = prompt(`Link RC Account "\${rcName}" (ID: \${rcId}) to which GCTTA Profile Name?`);
        if (!localName) return;
        
        fetch('/api/admin/update_player_profile', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({player_name: localName, ratings_central_id: rcId})
        }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                alert(`Successfully linked!`);
            } else {
                alert('Error linking account');
            }
        });
    }
</script>
