=== FILE: ./frontend/templates/Components/Admin/scripts/operations.html ===
<script>
    console.log("✅ Admin Operations Module Loaded");

    // Native Zero-Dependency WebAuthn Passkey Registration Helper
    function _adminB64urlToUint8Array(base64url) {
        const padding = '='.repeat((4 - (base64url.length % 4)) % 4);
        const base64 = (base64url + padding).replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
        const rawData = window.atob(base64);
        const outputArray = new Uint8Array(rawData.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < rawData.length; ++i) {
            outputArray[i] = rawData.charCodeAt(i);
        }
        return outputArray;
    }

    function _adminBufferToB64url(buffer) {
        const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
        let binary = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < bytes.byteLength; i++) {
            binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
        }
        return window.btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
    }

    window.registerAdminPasskey = async function() {
        if (window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1') {
            if (confirm("Apple Passkeys require 'localhost' instead of '127.0.0.1' when testing locally. Switch to localhost now?")) {
                window.location.href = window.location.href.replace('127.0.0.1', 'localhost');
            }
            return;
        }

        try {
            const optRes = await fetch('/api/auth/passkey/register-options', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({})
            });
            const options = await optRes.json();
            if (options.error) throw new Error(options.error);

            const publicKey = {
                challenge: _adminB64urlToUint8Array(options.challenge),
                rp: { name: options.rp.name },
                user: {
                    id: _adminB64urlToUint8Array(options.user.id),
                    name: options.user.name,
                    displayName: options.user.displayName
                },
                pubKeyCredParams: options.pubKeyCredParams,
                timeout: options.timeout || 60000,
                attestation: options.attestation || 'none',
                authenticatorSelection: options.authenticatorSelection
            };

            if (options.rp.id && options.rp.id !== '127.0.0.1') {
                publicKey.rp.id = options.rp.id;
            }

            const cred = await navigator.credentials.create({ publicKey });
            if (!cred) throw new Error("Passkey creation was cancelled.");

            const regResponse = {
                id: cred.id,
                rawId: _adminBufferToB64url(cred.rawId),
                type: cred.type,
                response: {
                    attestationObject: _adminBufferToB64url(cred.response.attestationObject),
                    clientDataJSON: _adminBufferToB64url(cred.response.clientDataJSON),
                    transports: cred.response.getTransports ? cred.response.getTransports() : ['internal', 'hybrid']
                }
            };

            const verifyRes = await fetch('/api/auth/passkey/register-verify', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ reg_response: regResponse })
            });
            const resData = await verifyRes.json();
            if (resData.success) {
                alert("✅ " + (resData.message || "Synced Passkey registered! You can now log in using Face ID / Touch ID across your Apple devices."));
            } else {
                throw new Error(resData.error || "Failed to verify passkey.");
            }
        } catch (err) {
            alert("Passkey Setup: " + (err.message || "Cancelled or failed."));
        }
    };

    window.applyRoleBasedUI = function() {
        const userRole = '{{ role }}'; 
        if (userRole === 'moderator' || userRole === 'admin') {
            const superElements = document.querySelectorAll('.super-admin-only');
            superElements.forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden', 'pointer-events-none'));
            console.log(`🔒 Role-Based UI Applied: Locked to ${userRole.toUpperCase()} level.`);
        }
    };

    window.loadSystemDiagnostics = async function() {
        window.applyRoleBasedUI();
        
        const vBox = document.getElementById('diag-views');
        const hBox = document.getElementById('diag-humans');
        const bBox = document.getElementById('diag-bots');
        const logTable = document.getElementById('visitor-log-table');
        
        if (vBox) vBox.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-lg"></i>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/traffic');
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if (vBox) vBox.innerText = data.views || 0;
            if (hBox) hBox.innerText = data.humans || 0;
            if (bBox) bBox.innerText = data.bots || 0;
            
            if (logTable) {
                if (!data.logs || data.logs.length === 0) {
                    logTable.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-6 text-slate-500 text-xs italic">No traffic logged yet today.</div>';
                } else {
                    logTable.innerHTML = data.logs.map(log => {
                        const botBadge = log.is_bot 
                            ? '<span class="bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest"><i class="fa-solid fa-robot mr-1"></i> Bot</span>' 
                            : '<span class="bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest"><i class="fa-solid fa-user mr-1"></i> Human</span>';
                        
                        return `
                        <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4 p-3 border-b border-white/5 text-xs text-slate-300 items-center hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">
                            <div class="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-500">${log.time}</div>
                            <div class="font-mono">${log.ip}</div>
                            <div class="font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                                ${log.device.includes('iPhone') ? '<i class="fa-brands fa-apple text-slate-400"></i>' : ''}
                                ${log.device.includes('Mac') ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-laptop text-slate-400"></i>' : ''}
                                ${log.device.includes('Windows') ? '<i class="fa-brands fa-windows text-slate-400"></i>' : ''}
                                ${log.device.includes('Android') ? '<i class="fa-brands fa-android text-slate-400"></i>' : ''}
                                ${log.device}
                            </div>
                            <div>${botBadge}</div>
                        </div>`;
                    }).join('');
                }
            }
            
        } catch(e) {
            if (vBox) vBox.innerText = 'Err';
        }
    };

    window.runMathDiagnostic = async function() {
        const p1 = document.getElementById('diag-p1-search').value.trim();
        const p2 = document.getElementById('diag-p2-search').value.trim();
        const s1 = document.getElementById('diag-s1').value.trim();
        const s2 = document.getElementById('diag-s2').value.trim();
        
        if (!p1 || !p2 || s1 === '' || s2 === '') return alert("Please fill all fields.");

        const btn = document.getElementById('diag-btn');
        const origText = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> CALCULATING...';
        btn.disabled = true;

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/glicko_calc', {
                method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ p1: p1, p2: p2, s1: s1, s2: s2 })
            });
            const out = await res.json();
            const box = document.getElementById('diag-results');
            box.classList.remove('hidden');
            
            if (out.success) {
                const r = out.data;
                const formatDelta = d => (d > 0 ? `+${d.toFixed(1)}` : d.toFixed(1));
                const colP1 = r.p1.delta > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-red-400';
                const colP2 = r.p2.delta > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-red-400';
                
                box.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-sky-400 font-black mb-2 uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px] border-b border-white/10 pb-2">Calculation Success</div>
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center bg-black/40 p-2 rounded">
                        <span class="text-slate-300 font-bold">${r.p1.name}</span>
                        <div class="text-right">
                            <span class="text-white">${Math.round(r.p1.new_rating)}</span>
                            <span class="text-[10px] ${colP1} ml-2 font-black">${formatDelta(r.p1.delta)}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center bg-black/40 p-2 rounded">
                        <span class="text-slate-300 font-bold">${r.p2.name}</span>
                        <div class="text-right">
                            <span class="text-white">${Math.round(r.p2.new_rating)}</span>
                            <span class="text-[10px] ${colP2} ml-2 font-black">${formatDelta(r.p2.delta)}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 mt-2 italic text-center">Using current active multiplier scales.</div>
                `;
            } else {
                box.innerHTML = `<span class="text-red-500">Error: ${out.error || "Unknown"}</span>`;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            alert("Diagnostic Request Failed.");
        } finally {
            btn.innerHTML = origText;
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    window.recalcRecent = async function() {
        if(!confirm("⚠️ Replay the last 3 weeks of matches chronologically to fix recent rating bugs?")) return;
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-recalc-recent');
        const origHtml = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i></div><div class="text-left text-white">Rebuilding recent history...</div></div>';
        btn.disabled = true;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/recalculate_recent', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("✅ Last 3 weeks recalculated successfully!");
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error: " + data.error);
            }
        } catch(e) {
            alert("Network error.");
        } finally {
            btn.innerHTML = origHtml;
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    window.recalcFull = async function() {
        if(!confirm("🚨 DANGER: This will wipe all historical ledgers and replay the ENTIRE club history chronologically. It may take 30+ seconds. Are you absolutely sure?")) return;
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-recalc-full');
        const origHtml = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center"><i class="fa-solid fa-radiation fa-spin"></i></div><div class="text-left text-white">Rebuilding entire database...</div></div>';
        btn.disabled = true;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/recalculate', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("✅ Full database recalculation complete!");
            } else {
                alert("❌ Error: " + data.error);
            }
        } catch(e) {
            alert("Network error. The process might still be running in the background.");
        } finally {
            btn.innerHTML = origHtml;
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    window.scanForErrors = async function() {
        const box = document.getElementById('error-results-box');
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-12"><i class="fa-solid fa-radar fa-spin text-red-500 text-3xl mb-4"></i><div class="text-xs text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Scanning entire database...</div></div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/date_errors');
            const errors = await res.json();
            
            if (errors.length === 0) {
                box.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check text-emerald-500 text-4xl mb-4"></i><div class="text-emerald-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">System Clean!</div><div class="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-2">No missing dates found.</div></div>';
                return;
            }
            
            box.innerHTML = errors.map(e => `
                <div class="bg-red-900/10 border border-red-500/20 p-3 rounded-lg mb-2 flex justify-between items-center">
                    <div>
                        <div class="text-red-400 font-black text-sm">${e.season} - ${e.division}</div>
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">${e.match_summary || ''}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="bg-red-950 px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] text-red-500 border border-red-900 font-mono">
                        Week ${e.week}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
            
        } catch (e) {
            box.innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-500 text-center py-10 text-sm">Network error during scan.</div>';
        }
    };

    window.upcomingSchedules = [];
    window.loadSchedules = async function() {
        const listEl = document.getElementById('schedules-list'); if(!listEl) return;
        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl text-red-500"></i></div>';
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/upcoming_schedules'); window.upcomingSchedules = await res.json();
            window.populateScheduleFilters(window.upcomingSchedules); window.renderSchedules();
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.populateScheduleFilters = function(data) {
        const years = new Set(); const seasons = new Set(); const divs = new Set();
        data.forEach(item => {
            const raw = item.season || "Unknown";
            const ym = raw.match(/\d{4}/); if (ym) years.add(ym[0]);
            const tm = raw.replace(/\d{4}/g, '').trim(); if (tm) seasons.add(tm);
            if (item.division) divs.add(item.division);
        });
        const buildOptions = (set, defaultText) => { let html = `<option value="All">${defaultText}</option>`; Array.from(set).sort().forEach(val => { html += `<option value="${val}">${val}</option>`; }); return html; };
        if(document.getElementById('schedule-year-filter')) document.getElementById('schedule-year-filter').innerHTML = buildOptions(years, "All Years");
        if(document.getElementById('schedule-season-filter')) document.getElementById('schedule-season-filter').innerHTML = buildOptions(seasons, "All Seasons");
        if(document.getElementById('schedule-div-filter')) document.getElementById('schedule-div-filter').innerHTML = buildOptions(divs, "All Divisions");
    };

    window.renderSchedules = function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('schedules-list'); if (!list) return;
        const yF = document.getElementById('schedule-year-filter')?.value || 'All'; const sF = document.getElementById('schedule-season-filter')?.value || 'All'; const dF = document.getElementById('schedule-div-filter')?.value || 'All';
        const filtered = window.upcomingSchedules.filter(item => {
            const raw = item.season || "";
            return (yF === 'All' || raw.includes(yF)) && (sF === 'All' || raw.includes(sF)) && (dF === 'All' || item.division === dF);
        });
        if (filtered.length === 0) { list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-slate-500 text-sm italic">No matches found.</div>'; return; }
        filtered.sort((a,b) => (a.date_text || '').localeCompare(b.date_text || ''));
        let html = '';
        filtered.forEach(s => {
            html += `
            <div class="bg-black/30 border border-white/5 p-4 rounded-lg flex justify-between items-center group transition-colors hover:bg-slate-800">
                <div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
                        <span class="bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-white/10">${s.season || 'Unknown'}</span>
                        <span class="bg-red-900/30 text-red-400 border border-red-500/20 text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">${s.division}</span>
                        <span class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono"><i class="fa-regular fa-calendar mr-1"></i>${s.date_text}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-sm font-black text-white">${s.home_team} <span class="text-slate-500 text-xs font-normal mx-2 text-[10px] uppercase">vs</span> ${s.away_team}</div>
                </div>
                <button onclick="window.deleteSchedule('${s.id}')" title="Delete Match" class="text-red-500 hover:text-white bg-red-900/20 hover:bg-red-600 border border-red-500/20 rounded px-3 py-2 transition opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 shadow"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
            </div>`;
        });
        list.innerHTML = html;
    };

    window.deleteSchedule = async function(id) {
        if(!confirm("Delete this match from the schedule?")) return;
        try { const res = await fetch('/api/admin/delete_schedule', { method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({schedule_id: id}) }); if((await res.json()).success) window.loadSchedules(); } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.uploadPdfSchedule = async function(event) {
        if (event) event.preventDefault();
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdf-file-upload'); 
        if (!fileInput || !fileInput.files || fileInput.files.length === 0) return alert("Select a PDF first.");
        const yearStr = document.getElementById('pdf-year') ? document.getElementById('pdf-year').value : new Date().getFullYear();
        const seasonStr = document.getElementById('pdf-season') ? document.getElementById('pdf-season').value : 'Summer';
        const division = document.getElementById('pdf-division') ? document.getElementById('pdf-division').value : 'Unknown Division';
        const formData = new FormData(); formData.append('pdf', fileInput.files[0]); formData.append('season', `${seasonStr} ${yearStr}`); formData.append('division', division);
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/upload_schedule', { method: 'POST', body: formData }); const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { alert(`✅ Extracted ${data.teams_found} teams and ${data.matches_found} matchups.`); window.loadSchedules(); } else alert("❌ Failed: " + data.error);
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.submitManualMatch = async function() {
        const p1 = document.getElementById('manual-p1').value; const p2 = document.getElementById('manual-p2').value; const date = document.getElementById('manual-date').value; const scores = document.getElementById('manual-scores').value;
        if (!p1 || !p2 || !date) return alert("Fields missing.");
        if (!confirm(`Create match: ${p1} vs ${p2}?`)) return;
        try { const res = await fetch('/api/admin/manual_match', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ p1: p1, p2: p2, date: date, scores: scores }) }); if ((await res.json()).success) alert("✅ Match injected!"); } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.searchHistory = async function() {
        const q = document.getElementById('history-search').value.trim(); const dF = document.getElementById('hist-div-filter')?.value || 'All'; const sF = document.getElementById('hist-source-filter')?.value || 'All';
        const list = document.getElementById('historyList'); list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10 text-indigo-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i></div>';
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/history?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&source=${encodeURIComponent(sF)}`); let matches = await res.json();
            if (dF !== 'All') matches = matches.filter(m => m.division.toLowerCase() === dF.toLowerCase());
            if (matches.length === 0) { list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic">No matches found.</div>'; return; }
            let html = '';
            matches.forEach(m => {
                const sB = m.source === 'Spreadsheet' ? `<span class="bg-green-900/30 text-green-400 border border-green-500/20 text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-excel mr-1"></i>Sheet</span>` : `<span class="bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/20 text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded"><i class="fa-solid fa-tablet mr-1"></i>App</span>`;
                html += `
                <div class="bg-black/30 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 mb-3 flex flex-col hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors">
                    <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 w-full">
                        <div class="flex-1 w-full">
                            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-1"><span class="bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono">ID: ${m.match_id}</span><span class="bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-400 text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">${m.division}</span>${sB}</div>
                            <div class="text-lg font-black text-white">${m.p1} <span class="text-slate-500 mx-2 text-sm">vs</span> ${m.p2}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-900/80 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/10 shrink-0">
                            <div class="flex flex-col w-14"><span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 text-center">S1</span><input type="number" id="s1-${m.id}" value="${m.s1}" class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold text-center rounded py-1 text-sm"></div>
                            <span class="text-slate-600 font-black">-</span>
                            <div class="flex flex-col w-14"><span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 text-center">S2</span><input type="number" id="s2-${m.id}" value="${m.s2}" class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold text-center rounded py-1 text-sm"></div>
                            <div class="flex flex-col w-28 ml-2"><span class="text-[8px] text-slate-500 text-center">Date</span><input type="text" id="date-${m.id}" value="${m.date}" class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold text-center rounded py-1 text-sm"></div>
                            <button onclick="window.adminSaveHistoryEdit('${m.p1.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${m.p2.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${m.date}', '${m.id}')" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg mt-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk"></i></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) {}
    };

            if (errors.length === 0) { dList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-600 text-center py-10 italic">No missing dates found.</div>'; return; }

                </div>`;
            });
            dList.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) {}
    };

=== FILE: ./frontend/templates/Components/Admin/scripts/accounts.html ===
<script>
    console.log("✅ Admin Accounts Module Loaded");

    let playerDirectoryData = [];
    window.loadPlayerDirectory = async function() { 
        try { 
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/player_directory'); 
            playerDirectoryData = await res.json(); 
        } catch(e) {
            console.error("Failed to fetch player directory:", e);
        } 
    };

    function attachPlayerSearch(inputId, suggId, hiddenId = null) {
        const input = document.getElementById(inputId); const sugg = document.getElementById(suggId);
        if (!input || !sugg) return;
        input.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const q = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
            if (q.length < 1) { sugg.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
            if (playerDirectoryData.length === 0) return;
            const matches = playerDirectoryData.filter(p => (p.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
            if (matches.length === 0) { sugg.innerHTML = '<div class="px-3 py-2 text-slate-400 text-xs italic">No players found</div>'; sugg.classList.remove('hidden'); return; }
            sugg.innerHTML = matches.slice(0, 8).map(p => `<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-blue-600/30 cursor-pointer text-sm text-white font-bold border-b border-white/5" onclick="document.getElementById('${inputId}').value = '${p.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}'; ${hiddenId ? `document.getElementById('${hiddenId}').value = '${p.id}';` : ''} document.getElementById('${suggId}').classList.add('hidden');">${p.name} <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono ml-2">${p.id}</span></div>`).join('');
            sugg.classList.remove('hidden');
        });
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target !== input && e.target !== sugg) sugg.classList.add('hidden'); });
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        window.setAdminCategory('ops'); 
        attachPlayerSearch('override-search', 'override-suggestions', 'override-id');
        attachPlayerSearch('merge-bad-search', 'merge-bad-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('merge-good-search', 'merge-good-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('diag-p1-search', 'diag-p1-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('diag-p2-search', 'diag-p2-suggestions');
        attachPlayerSearch('manual-p1', 'sugg-p1');
        attachPlayerSearch('manual-p2', 'sugg-p2');
        attachPlayerSearch('profile-edit-search', 'profile-edit-sugg');

        const searchInput = document.getElementById('profile-edit-search');
        if(searchInput) {
            searchInput.addEventListener('blur', () => { setTimeout(() => { const playerName = searchInput.value.trim(); if(playerName) window.loadProfileForEditing(playerName); }, 200); });
        }
    });

    window.loadProfileForEditing = async function(playerName) {
        const rcInput = document.getElementById('profile-rc-id'); const saveBtn = document.getElementById('profile-save-btn'); const syncBtn = document.getElementById('profile-sync-btn');
        if(!rcInput) return; rcInput.disabled = true; saveBtn.disabled = true; syncBtn.disabled = true; rcInput.value = "Loading...";
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/player_profile/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}`); const data = await res.json();
            rcInput.value = data.ratings_central_id || ''; rcInput.disabled = false; saveBtn.disabled = false; syncBtn.disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('rc-sync-status').innerText = data.last_synced ? `Last RC Sync: ${data.last_synced}` : '';
        } catch(e) { rcInput.value = ""; }
    };

    window.savePlayerProfile = async function() {
        const playerName = document.getElementById('profile-edit-search').value.trim(); const rcId = document.getElementById('profile-rc-id').value.trim();
        if(!playerName) return alert("Please select a player first.");
        const btn = document.getElementById('profile-save-btn'); const origText = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> SAVING...'; btn.disabled = true;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/update_player_profile', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ player_name: playerName, ratings_central_id: rcId }) });
            const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { alert(`✅ Profile updated! RC data has been fetched.`); window.loadProfileForEditing(playerName); } else alert("❌ Failed to update profile.");
        } catch(e) { alert("Network Error"); } finally { btn.innerHTML = origText; btn.disabled = false; }
    };

    window.forceRCSync = async function() {
        const playerName = document.getElementById('profile-edit-search').value.trim(); const rcId = document.getElementById('profile-rc-id').value.trim();
        if(!playerName || !rcId) return alert("Must select a player and have an RC ID set.");
        const btn = document.getElementById('profile-sync-btn'); const origText = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>'; btn.disabled = true;
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/sync_rc/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}`, { method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({rc_id: rcId}) });
            const data = await res.json();
            if(data.success) { alert(`✅ Background scraper started! The full stats for ${playerName} will be synced to Firebase shortly.`); window.loadProfileForEditing(playerName); }
            else alert("❌ " + data.error);
        } catch(e) { alert("Network Error"); } finally { btn.innerHTML = origText; btn.disabled = false; }
    };

    let globalVerifiedAccounts = [];

    function findBestMatchIndex(searchName, options) {
        if (!searchName) return 0;
        searchName = searchName.toLowerCase().trim();
        let bestIdx = 0; let bestScore = 0;
        for (let i = 1; i < options.length; i++) { 
            const optName = options[i].text.toLowerCase();
            if (optName === searchName) return i; 
            let score = 0; const searchParts = searchName.split(/\s+/); const optParts = optName.split(/\s+/);
            searchParts.forEach(sp => { if (sp.length > 2 && optName.includes(sp)) score += 1; if (optParts.includes(sp)) score += 1.5; });
            if (searchParts.length === 1 && score >= 1 && score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; bestIdx = i; } 
            else if (searchParts.length > 1 && score >= 2 && score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; bestIdx = i; }
        }
        return bestIdx;
    }

    window.searchRCForPlayer = async function(uid, name, inputId, userClub = '') {
        const resultsBox = document.getElementById(`rc-results-${uid}`);
        if(!resultsBox) return;
        resultsBox.classList.remove('hidden');
        resultsBox.innerHTML = '<div class="text-[9px] text-sky-400 text-center py-4"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-xl mb-2"></i><br>Live Querying Ratings Central...</div>';
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/search_rc', { method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({name: name, club: userClub}) });
            const data = await res.json();
            
            if(!data.success || data.results.length === 0) {
                resultsBox.innerHTML = '<div class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 text-center py-3">No players found on RC.</div>';
                setTimeout(() => resultsBox.classList.add('hidden'), 4000);
                return;
            }
            
            let html = '';
            data.results.forEach(r => {
                let clubHtml = ''; let clubHighlight = '';
                if (r.club && r.club !== 'Unknown Club') {
                    const isMatch = userClub && r.club.toLowerCase().includes(userClub.toLowerCase());
                    const clubColor = isMatch ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-slate-400';
                    const clubIcon = isMatch ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i>' : '<i class="fa-solid fa-house-flag"></i>';
                    clubHtml = `<div class="text-[9px] ${clubColor} font-bold mt-1 uppercase tracking-widest">${clubIcon} Club: ${r.club}</div>`;
                    if (isMatch) clubHighlight = 'border-emerald-500/50 bg-emerald-900/20';
                }
                html += `
                <div onclick="document.getElementById('${inputId}').value = '${r.id}'; document.getElementById('rc-results-${uid}').classList.add('hidden');" class="cursor-pointer bg-slate-800 hover:bg-sky-600/30 border border-white/10 hover:border-sky-500/50 ${clubHighlight} rounded-lg p-3 transition text-left group">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-1">
                        <div class="text-sm text-white font-bold group-hover:text-sky-400 transition">${r.name}</div>
                        <div class="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-black tracking-widest font-mono bg-emerald-900/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded shadow-inner border border-emerald-500/20">${r.rating}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                        <span class="text-[9px] text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold bg-black/40 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-white/5">ID: ${r.id}</span>
                        <span class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest"><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot mr-1"></i> ${r.location}</span>
                    </div>
                    ${clubHtml}
                    <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 leading-tight mt-1.5"><span class="text-slate-600 font-bold uppercase mr-1">Played:</span> ${r.recent_opponents}</div>
                </div>`;
            });
            resultsBox.innerHTML = html;
        } catch(e) {
            resultsBox.innerHTML = '<div class="text-[9px] text-red-500 text-center py-2">Error connecting to RC.</div>';
            setTimeout(() => resultsBox.classList.add('hidden'), 3000);
        }
    };

    window.loadAccountManagementData = async function() {
        if (playerDirectoryData.length === 0) await window.loadPlayerDirectory();
        
        const pendingList = document.getElementById('pendingAccountsList'); 
        if (pendingList) {
            pendingList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-10"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl text-emerald-500"></i></div>';
            try {
                const pendingRes = await fetch('/api/admin/pending_accounts'); const rawData = await pendingRes.json();
                const data = Array.isArray(rawData) ? rawData : (rawData.pending_users || []);
                if (data.length === 0) { pendingList.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-xl">Awesome! No pending accounts to approve.</div>'; } 
                else { window.renderPendingAccounts(data, pendingList); }
            } catch(e) {
                console.error("Error loading pending accounts", e);
            }
        }
        
        try {
            const allUsersRes = await fetch('/api/admin/all_users'); 
            const allUsersData = await allUsersRes.json();
            
            if (allUsersData.success) {
                globalVerifiedAccounts = allUsersData.users;
                
                try { await window.renderVerifiedAccounts(); } catch(e) { console.error("Error rendering verified accounts", e); }
                try { window.renderMergeDropdowns(globalVerifiedAccounts); } catch(e) { console.error("Error rendering merge dropdowns", e); }
                
                const missingList = document.getElementById('missingRCList');
                if (missingList) {
                    try { window.renderMissingRC(globalVerifiedAccounts, missingList); } catch(e) { console.error("Error rendering missing RC list", e); }
                }
            }
        } catch(e) {
            console.error("Error fetching all users", e);
        }
    };

    window.renderPendingAccounts = function(users, container) {
        container.innerHTML = '';
        let playerOptions = '<option value="">-- Select Official Player --</option>';
        playerDirectoryData.sort((a,b) => (a.name||'').localeCompare(b.name||'')).forEach(p => { playerOptions += `<option value="${p.name.replace(/'/g, "&#39;")}">${p.name}</option>`; });
        users.forEach(a => {
            const hasSuggestion = a.suggested_rc_id && a.suggested_rc_id !== "";
            const defaultRcVal = hasSuggestion ? a.suggested_rc_id : '';
            const suggestionBadge = hasSuggestion ? `<div id="badge-${a.uid}" class="text-[9px] text-amber-400 font-bold mt-1 bg-amber-900/30 border border-amber-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded inline-block animate-pulse"><i class="fa-solid fa-lightbulb mr-1"></i> User Typed: ${a.suggested_rc_id}</div>` : `<div id="badge-${a.uid}" class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Optional: Link RC Profile</div>`;

            container.innerHTML += `
            <div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-6 shadow-lg mb-4 hover:border-emerald-500/30 transition-colors">
                <div class="w-full md:w-1/2">
                    <div class="font-black text-white text-xl">${a.name}</div>
                    <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono mt-1">${a.email}</div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mt-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-table-tennis-paddle-ball mr-1"></i> ${a.club || 'No Club'}</div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">DOB: ${a.dob}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex flex-col bg-black/30 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 w-full md:w-1/2">
                    <label class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest block">Link to App Fixtures</label>
                    <div id="fixture-sugg-badge-${a.uid}" class="mt-1 mb-1.5 min-h-[20px]"></div>
                    <select id="link-player-${a.uid}" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-sm text-white focus:border-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-inner mb-4">${playerOptions}</select>
                    <div class="flex gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-white/5">
                        <button onclick="window.rejectPendingAccount('${a.uid}')" class="w-1/3 bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-500 hover:text-white border border-red-500/30 font-bold py-3 rounded-lg text-xs transition shadow flex justify-center items-center gap-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Reject</button>
                        <button onclick="window.linkAccount('${a.uid}')" class="w-2/3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black py-3 rounded-lg text-sm transition shadow flex justify-center items-center gap-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i> Approve & Link</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>`;
        });
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            users.forEach(a => {
                const select = document.getElementById(`link-player-${a.uid}`);
                if(select) {
                    const bestIndex = findBestMatchIndex(a.name, select.options);
                    if (bestIndex > 0) {
                        select.selectedIndex = bestIndex;
                        document.getElementById(`fixture-sugg-badge-${a.uid}`).innerHTML = `<div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-bold inline-block bg-emerald-900/30 border border-emerald-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded shadow-inner"><i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-1"></i> AI Suggests: ${select.options[bestIndex].value}</div>`;
                    } else {
                        document.getElementById(`fixture-sugg-badge-${a.uid}`).innerHTML = `<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info mr-1"></i> No Match Found</div>`;
                    }
                }
            });
        }, 150);
    }

    window.renderMissingRC = function(users, container) {
        container.innerHTML = '';
        const missingUsers = users.filter(u => !u.ratings_central_id || u.ratings_central_id.trim() === '');
        if (missingUsers.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-xl">Awesome! All approved users have a Ratings Central ID.</div>'; return; }

        missingUsers.forEach(u => {
            const rcSearchLink = `
                <button onclick="window.searchRCForPlayer('${u.id}', '${u.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', 'link-rc-${u.id}', '${(u.club || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="text-[10px] bg-sky-900/30 hover:bg-sky-600 border border-sky-500/30 text-sky-400 hover:text-white font-bold uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition flex items-center gap-1.5 w-full justify-center shadow">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Live Search RC Database
                </button>
                <div id="rc-results-${u.id}" class="hidden mt-2 bg-slate-950 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 shadow-inner custom-scrollbar"></div>
            `;
            container.innerHTML += `
            <div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start gap-6 shadow-lg mb-4 hover:border-sky-500/30 transition-colors">
                <div class="w-full md:w-1/3">
                    <div class="font-black text-white text-lg">${u.name}</div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-widest"><i class="fa-solid fa-link mr-1"></i> Linked: <span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">${u.linked_player_name || 'N/A'}</span></div>
                    <div class="text-[9px] text-sky-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mt-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-table-tennis-paddle-ball mr-1"></i> ${u.club || 'No Club'}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex flex-col bg-black/30 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 w-full md:w-2/3">
                    <label class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest block mb-2">Ratings Central ID</label>
                    <div class="flex gap-2">
                        <input type="text" id="link-rc-${u.id}" placeholder="e.g. 123456" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-sky-500 font-mono shadow-inner">
                        <button onclick="window.linkRCProfile('${u.id}', '${(u.linked_player_name || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="bg-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-500 text-white font-black px-4 rounded-lg text-sm transition shadow whitespace-nowrap">Save RC Link</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="mt-3 border-t border-white/5 pt-3">${rcSearchLink}</div>
                </div>
            </div>`;
        });
    }

    window.linkAccount = async function(uid) {
        const playerName = document.getElementById(`link-player-${uid}`).value;
        if (!playerName) return alert("Please select an official player name to link.");
        if (!confirm(`Approve account and link to ${playerName}?`)) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/approve_account', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: uid, player_name: playerName, ratings_central_id: '' }) });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { alert("✅ Account approved! Check Section 2 to link their RC Profile."); window.loadAccountManagementData(); } 
            else { alert("❌ Failed to link account: " + data.error); }
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.linkRCProfile = async function(uid, playerName) {
        const rcId = document.getElementById(`link-rc-${uid}`).value.trim();
        if (!rcId) return alert("Please enter an RC ID or select one from the search.");
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/update_verified_link', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ uid: uid, type: 'rc', value: rcId }) });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { 
                alert("✅ RC Profile linked! Pulling stats in the background...");
                fetch(`/api/admin/sync_rc/${encodeURIComponent(playerName)}`, { method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({rc_id: rcId}) });
                window.loadAccountManagementData(); 
            } else alert("❌ Error updating link.");
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.rejectPendingAccount = async function(uid) {
        if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to REJECT and DELETE this pending registration?\n\nThe user will have to sign up again.`)) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/reject_pending', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ uid: uid }) });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { alert("🗑️ Registration rejected and deleted."); window.loadAccountManagementData(); } else alert("❌ Failed to reject account.");
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    // --- UPGRADED ASYNC FUNCTION WITH DIRECTORY FAILSAFE ---
    window.renderVerifiedAccounts = async function() {
        const list = document.getElementById('verified-accounts-list'); 
        if (!list) return;

        // Failsafe: Ensure Player Directory is fully loaded before trying to build the dropdowns
        if (playerDirectoryData.length === 0) {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-6 text-emerald-500 italic text-sm"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Loading player directory...</div>';
            await window.loadPlayerDirectory();
        }

        const searchQ = (document.getElementById('account-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
        const filtered = globalVerifiedAccounts.filter(u => (u.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQ) || (u.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQ) || (u.linked_player_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQ));
        if (filtered.length === 0) { list.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-6 text-slate-500 italic text-sm">No accounts found.</div>'; return; }

        let playerOptions = '<option value="">-- No Linked Profile --</option>';
        if (playerDirectoryData && playerDirectoryData.length > 0) {
            playerDirectoryData.sort((a,b) => (a.name||'').localeCompare(b.name||'')).forEach(p => { playerOptions += `<option value="${p.name.replace(/'/g, "&#39;")}">${p.name}</option>`; });
        }

        let html = '';
        filtered.sort((a,b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')).forEach(u => {
            let userSpecificOptions = playerOptions;
            if(u.linked_player_name) userSpecificOptions = userSpecificOptions.replace(`value="${u.linked_player_name}"`, `value="${u.linked_player_name}" selected`);
            const tierBadge = u.tier === 'gold' ? '<span class="bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-500 border border-yellow-500/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase ml-2 shadow-[0_0_5px_rgba(234,179,8,0.3)]">GOLD</span>' : u.tier === 'silver' ? '<span class="bg-slate-300/20 text-slate-300 border border-slate-400/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase ml-2 shadow-[0_0_5px_rgba(203,213,225,0.3)]">SILVER</span>' : '';
            
            html += `
            <div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-white/5 shadow-lg mb-4 hover:border-blue-500/30 transition-colors">
                <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 border-b border-white/5 pb-4 mb-4">
                    <div>
                        <div class="font-black text-white text-lg flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2">${u.name} ${tierBadge} <span class="hidden bg-sky-900/30 text-sky-400 border border-sky-500/30 text-[9px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase tracking-widest" id="mod-badge-${u.uid || u.id}">MODERATOR</span></div>
                        <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono mt-1">${u.email}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        <button onclick="window.sendAdminPasswordReset('${u.email}')" class="bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-white/10 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] uppercase font-bold transition shadow"><i class="fa-solid fa-envelope mr-1"></i> Send Password Reset</button>
                        <button onclick="window.toggleModerator('${u.email}', this)" class="bg-sky-900/30 hover:bg-sky-600 text-sky-400 hover:text-white border border-sky-500/30 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] uppercase font-bold transition shadow"><i class="fa-solid fa-shield mr-1"></i> Toggle Mod</button>
                        <button onclick="window.deleteUserAccount('${u.id}')" class="bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-500 hover:text-white border border-red-500/30 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] uppercase font-bold transition shadow"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Delete Account</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 bg-black/20 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5">
                    <div>
                        <label class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest mb-2 block"><i class="fa-solid fa-table-tennis-paddle-ball mr-1"></i> Linked Fixture Profile</label>
                        <div class="flex gap-2">
                            <select id="edit-link-${u.id}" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500">${userSpecificOptions}</select>
                            <button onclick="window.updateVerifiedLink('${u.id}', 'player')" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs shadow transition"><i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk"></i></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest mb-1 block"><i class="fa-solid fa-globe mr-1"></i> Ratings Central ID</label>
                        <div class="flex gap-2">
                            <input type="text" id="edit-rc-${u.id}" value="${u.ratings_central_id || ''}" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">
                            <button onclick="window.updateVerifiedLink('${u.id}', 'rc')" class="bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-black font-black px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs shadow transition"><i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk"></i></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest mb-1 block"><i class="fa-solid fa-medal mr-1"></i> Membership Tier</label>
                        <div class="flex gap-2">
                            <select id="edit-tier-${u.id}" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-yellow-500">
                                <option value="standard" ${!u.tier || u.tier==='standard'?'selected':''}>Standard</option>
                                <option value="silver" ${u.tier==='silver'?'selected':''}>Silver</option>
                                <option value="gold" ${u.tier==='gold'?'selected':''}>Gold</option>
                            </select>
                            <button onclick="window.updateUserTier('${u.id}', document.getElementById('edit-tier-${u.id}').value)" class="bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-500 text-black font-black px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs shadow transition"><i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk"></i></button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>`;
        });
        list.innerHTML = html;
        
        fetch(`/api/admin/users`).then(r=>r.json()).then(users => {
            filtered.forEach(u => {
                if(users.find(x => x.email.toLowerCase() === (u.email || '').toLowerCase())) {
                    const badge = document.getElementById(`mod-badge-${u.uid || u.id}`);
                    if(badge) badge.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
            });
        }).catch(e => {});
    };

    window.updateVerifiedLink = async function(uid, type, forceVal = null) {
        let val = forceVal === null ? document.getElementById(`edit-${type === 'player' ? 'link' : 'rc'}-${uid}`).value : forceVal;
        if (val === 'DELINK') { if (!confirm(`Delink this user's profile?`)) return; val = ''; }
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/update_verified_link', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ uid: uid, type: type, value: val }) });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) { alert("✅ Link updated!"); if (val !== '') window.loadAccountManagementData(); } else alert("❌ Error updating link.");
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.updateUserTier = async function(uid, tier) {
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/update_user_tier', { method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({uid: uid, tier: tier}) });
            if((await res.json()).success) { alert(`✅ Tier updated!`); window.loadAccountManagementData(); }
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.toggleModerator = async function(email, btn) {
        if(!confirm(`Toggle moderator powers for ${email}?`)) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/toggle_moderator', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email: email }) });
            if ((await res.json()).success) { alert(`✅ User role updated!`); window.loadAccountManagementData(); }
        } catch(e) {}
    };

    window.deleteUserAccount = async function(uid) {
        if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to completely delete this account?")) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/delete_user', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: uid }) });
            if ((await res.json()).success) window.loadAccountManagementData();
        } catch (err) {}
    };

    window.renderMergeDropdowns = function(users) {
        const primary = document.getElementById('primaryAccountSelect'); const duplicate = document.getElementById('duplicateAccountSelect');
        if (!primary || !duplicate) return;
        let options = '<option value="">-- Select Account --</option>';
        users.sort((a,b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')).forEach(u => { options += `<option value="${u.id}">${u.name} (${u.email})</option>`; });
        primary.innerHTML = options; duplicate.innerHTML = options;
    };

    window.submitAccountMerge = async function() {
        const pId = document.getElementById('primaryAccountSelect').value; const dId = document.getElementById('duplicateAccountSelect').value;
        if (!pId || !dId || pId === dId) return alert("Select two different accounts.");
        if (!confirm("Are you ABSOLUTELY SURE? The duplicate account will be permanently deleted.")) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/merge_accounts', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ primary_user_id: pId, duplicate_user_id: dId }) });
            if ((await res.json()).success) { alert("✅ Accounts merged!"); window.loadAccountManagementData(); }
        } catch (err) {}
    };
    
    window.triggerMasterRCSync = async function() {
        if (!confirm("⚠️ WARNING: This will download thousands of Australian table tennis players from Ratings Central to your Firebase database and Google Sheets.\n\nThis process takes 2-3 minutes to run in the background.\n\nAre you sure you want to proceed?")) return;
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/trigger_master_rc_sync', { method: 'POST' });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("🚀 " + data.message + "\n\nYou can continue using the portal while it runs.");
            } else {
                alert("❌ Failed to start sync.");
            }
        } catch (e) {
            alert("Network Error while starting sync.");
        }
    };
</script>

    const firebaseConfig = { apiKey: "AIzaSyDq05wFsKLHtt8G0Z5yLOG9pIk9UX24S4o", authDomain: "gctta-stats-live.firebaseapp.com", projectId: "gctta-stats-live", storageBucket: "gctta-stats-live.firebasestorage.app", messagingSenderId: "1070856258556", appId: "1:1070856258556:web:be438e04c1941abc3c6879" };
    
    // ==========================================
    // CRITICAL FIX: INSTANT UI UNBLOCKING
    // Since Flask (@login_required) already verified the session to render this page,
    // we instantly kill the loading screen without waiting for Firebase Auth to boot up!
    // ==========================================
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const loaders = ['loading-screen', 'auth-loading', 'session-loader', 'loading'];
        loaders.forEach(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
        });
        
        // Automatically trigger the data load for the active tab instantly
        if (document.getElementById('admin-approvals') && !document.getElementById('admin-approvals').classList.contains('hidden')) {
            if(typeof window.loadAccountManagementData === 'function') window.loadAccountManagementData();
        }
    });

    try {
        const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig); 
        const db = getFirestore(app);
        const auth = getAuth(app);
        
        window.sendAdminPasswordReset = function(email) {
            if(!confirm(`Send a password reset email to ${email}?`)) return;
            sendPasswordResetEmail(auth, email)
                .then(() => alert(`✅ Password reset email sent securely to ${email} via Firebase.`))
                .catch(error => alert(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`));
        };

        window.setFormatOverride = async function(fixtureId, formatType) {
            try { await fetch('/api/admin/set_fixture_format', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fixture_id: fixtureId, format_type: formatType }) }); } catch(e) {}
        };
        
        onSnapshot(query(collection(db, "fixture_schedule")), (snapshot) => {
            const liveList = document.getElementById("live-list"); const scheduledList = document.getElementById("scheduled-list");
            let liveHtml = ""; let schedHtml = "";
            snapshot.forEach((doc) => {
                const m = doc.data(); const id = doc.id;
                const p1 = (m.home_players && m.home_players.length > 0) ? m.home_players[0] : m.home_team;
                const p2 = (m.away_players && m.away_players.length > 0) ? m.away_players[0] : m.away_team;
                if (m.match_status === "Live" || m.match_status === "Paused" || m.live_home_score > 0 || m.live_away_score > 0) {
                    const s1 = m.live_home_sets || 0; const s2 = m.live_away_sets || 0;
                    liveHtml += `<div class="bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-blue-500/30 shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden mb-4"><div class="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-blue-500 text-white text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 py-1 rounded-bl-lg animate-pulse">ACTIVE NOW</div><div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 mt-1"><span class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">Table ${m.table} • ${m.division}</span></div><div class="text-xl font-black text-white mb-4">${p1} <span class="text-slate-500 mx-2 text-sm">vs</span> ${p2}</div><div class="flex items-center gap-4 bg-black/40 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 mb-4 shadow-inner"><div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">Adjust Final Score:</div><input type="number" id="live-s1-${id}" value="${s1}" class="w-14 bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-2 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg"><span class="text-slate-500 font-black">-</span><input type="number" id="live-s2-${id}" value="${s2}" class="w-14 bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-white font-bold font-mono text-center rounded py-2 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none text-lg"></div><div class="flex gap-3"><button onclick="window.forceFinishLive('${id}', '${p1.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${p2.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02] shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(37,99,235,0.4)] flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-flag-checkered"></i> Force Finish</button><button onclick="window.wipeLiveMatch('${id}')" class="bg-red-900/50 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-400 hover:text-white border border-red-500/30 font-bold py-2.5 px-4 rounded-lg text-sm transition-colors" title="Wipe Error"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button></div></div>`;
                } else if (m.match_status === "Scheduled" || !m.match_status) {
                    const formatVal = m.format_override || "Auto";
                    schedHtml += `<div class="bg-slate-800/80 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 shadow relative"><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 border-b border-white/5 pb-1 flex justify-between"><span>Table ${m.table}</span><span class="text-blue-400">${m.division}</span></div><div class="font-bold text-white text-sm mt-2">${m.home_team} <br><span class="text-slate-500 text-xs font-normal">vs</span><br> ${m.away_team}</div><div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-white/5"><label class="block text-[9px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Match Format</label><select onchange="window.setFormatOverride('${id}', this.value)" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 text-slate-300 font-bold text-xs rounded py-2 px-2 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none"><option value="Auto" ${formatVal === 'Auto' ? 'selected' : ''}>Auto-Detect</option><option value="2v2" ${formatVal === '2v2' ? 'selected' : ''}>Force 2v2 (5 Matches)</option><option value="3v3" ${formatVal === '3v3' ? 'selected' : ''}>Force 3v3 (11 Matches)</option></select></div></div>`;
                }
            });
            if (liveList) liveList.innerHTML = liveHtml || '<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-2 text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/30">No matches currently active.</div>';
            if (scheduledList) scheduledList.innerHTML = schedHtml || '<div class="col-span-1 md:col-span-3 text-slate-500 text-center py-10 italic border border-white/5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/30">No scheduled matches pending.</div>';
        });

        window.forceFinishLive = async function(id, p1, p2) {
            const s1 = document.getElementById(`live-s1-${id}`).value; const s2 = document.getElementById(`live-s2-${id}`).value;
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/admin/force_finish_live', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: id, s1: s1, s2: s2 }) }); if ((await res.json()).success) alert("✅ Forced finished!"); } catch(e) {}
        };

        window.wipeLiveMatch = async function(id) {
            try { const res = await fetch('/api/admin/wipe_live', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: id }) }); if ((await res.json()).success) alert("🗑️ Wiped!"); } catch(e) {}
        };
    } catch(err) { console.warn("Firebase Live Connection failed.", err); }
</script>

=== FILE: ./frontend/templates/Components/Admin/scripts/ui_core.html ===
<script>
    console.log("✅ Admin UI Core Loaded");

    const inactivityTime = function () {
        let time; window.onload = resetTimer; document.onmousemove = resetTimer; document.onkeypress = resetTimer; document.ontouchstart = resetTimer; document.onclick = resetTimer;
        function logout() { alert("Logged out due to inactivity."); window.location.href = '/logout'; }
        function resetTimer() { clearTimeout(time); time = setTimeout(logout, 120000); }
    };
    inactivityTime();

    window.setAdminCategory = function(catId) {
        // THE FIX: Added 'diagnostics' to the list of main categories to manage
        ['ops', 'players', 'accounts', 'comms', 'system', 'diagnostics'].forEach(c => {
            const btn = document.getElementById(`cat-${c}`);
            if (btn) { btn.classList.remove('border-blue-500', 'text-blue-400'); btn.classList.add('border-transparent', 'text-slate-500'); }
            const sub = document.getElementById(`sub-${c}`);
            if (sub) sub.classList.add('hidden');
        });

        const targetBtn = document.getElementById(`cat-${catId}`);
        if (targetBtn) { targetBtn.classList.remove('border-transparent', 'text-slate-500'); targetBtn.classList.add('border-blue-500', 'text-blue-400'); }
        const targetSub = document.getElementById(`sub-${catId}`);
        if (targetSub) targetSub.classList.remove('hidden');

        // Link the main category tabs to their default sub-pages
        if (catId === 'ops') window.switchAdminTab('live');
        if (catId === 'players') window.switchAdminTab('players');
        if (catId === 'accounts') window.switchAdminTab('approvals');
        if (catId === 'comms') window.switchAdminTab('notices');
        if (catId === 'system') window.switchAdminTab('audit');
        // THE FIX: Tell the UI to open the actual diagnostics page!
        if (catId === 'diagnostics') window.switchAdminTab('diagnostics');
    };

    window.switchAdminTab = function(tabId) {
        const allTabs = ['live', 'manual', 'history', 'date-errors', 'tournaments', 'players', 'diagnostics', 'schedules', 'broadcast', 'sms-inbox', 'notices', 'community-mod', 'inbox-feedback', 'donations', 'audit', 'chat', 'approvals', 'directory', 'merge-accounts'];
        allTabs.forEach(t => {
            const section = document.getElementById(`admin-${t}`);
            if (section) section.classList.add('hidden');
            const btn = document.getElementById(`tab-btn-${t}`);
            if (btn) btn.className = "px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap transition-colors";
        });
        
        const targetSection = document.getElementById(`admin-${tabId}`);
        if (targetSection) targetSection.classList.remove('hidden');
        const targetBtn = document.getElementById(`tab-btn-${tabId}`);
        if (targetBtn) targetBtn.className = "px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-white/10 rounded-lg cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap transition-colors";

        if (tabId === 'inbox-feedback') window.loadPendingReports();
        if (tabId === 'date-errors') window.loadDateErrors();
        if (tabId === 'notices') window.loadAdminNotices();
        if (tabId === 'chat') window.loadAdminChat();
        if (tabId === 'donations') window.loadDonations();
        if (tabId === 'sms-inbox') window.loadSmsInbox();
        if (tabId === 'schedules') window.loadSchedules();
        if (['approvals', 'directory', 'merge-accounts'].includes(tabId)) window.loadAccountManagementData();
        if (tabId === 'history') { const f = document.getElementById('hist-div-filter'); if (f && f.options.length <= 1) fetch('/api/divisions').then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{d.forEach(div=>{f.innerHTML+=`<option value="${div}">${div}</option>`})}); }
        if (['players', 'diagnostics', 'manual'].includes(tabId) && typeof playerDirectoryData !== 'undefined' && playerDirectoryData.length === 0) window.loadPlayerDirectory();
        
        // Ensure Traffic data refreshes every time you open the Diagnostics tab
        if (tabId === 'diagnostics') { 
            fetch('/api/admin/get_rating_scales').then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{document.getElementById('math-k-win').value=d.k_win;document.getElementById('math-k-loss').value=d.k_loss;});
            if (typeof window.loadSystemDiagnostics === 'function') window.loadSystemDiagnostics();
        }
        
        if (tabId === 'audit') { window.loadAuditLogs(); window.loadAdminUsers(); window.loadTrafficStats(); }
    };

    window.forceRefresh = async function() {
        if (!confirm("Force the server to pull the latest data from the Master Google Sheet?")) return;
        const btn = document.querySelector('button[onclick="window.forceRefresh()"]'); const orig = btn ? btn.innerHTML : "FORCE REFRESH";
        if (btn) { btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> SYNCING...'; btn.disabled = true; }
        try { const res = await fetch('/api/refresh', { method: 'POST' }); const data = await res.json(); if (data.success) alert("✅ Sync triggered!"); else alert("❌ Sync failed."); } 
        catch(e) { alert("❌ Network Error."); } finally { if (btn) { btn.innerHTML = orig; btn.disabled = false; } }
    };
</script>

<script>
    document.getElementById('broadcast-input').addEventListener('input', function() {
        this.value = this.value.replace(/[^\x20-\x7E\n\r]/g, '');
        const msg = this.value || '[Your message will appear here]';
        const counter = document.getElementById('char-counter');
        
        counter.innerText = `${this.value.length} / 95 chars`;
        if (this.value.length >= 85) counter.className = "text-red-400 font-mono font-bold text-[10px] bg-red-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-red-500/30";
        else counter.className = "text-sky-400 font-mono text-[10px] bg-sky-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-sky-500/30";

        document.getElementById('broadcast-preview').innerText = `GCTTA Update: ${msg}\n\nView stats: gctta-stats.com.au\nReply STOP to opt out`;
    });
</script>
