            <div id="feedbackList" class="space-y-3">
                <div class="text-slate-600 text-center py-4 italic text-sm">No new feedback.</div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    let globalPlayersForDropdown = [];

    window.loadAccountManagementData = async function() {
        try {
            // Fetch pending accounts and players
            const pendingRes = await fetch('/api/admin/pending_accounts');
            const pendingData = await pendingRes.json();
            
            if (pendingData.success) {
                globalPlayersForDropdown = pendingData.players;
                renderPendingAccounts(pendingData.pending_users);
                populateAllPlayerDropdowns(pendingData.players);
            }

            // Fetch all users for the merge dropdown
            const allUsersRes = await fetch('/api/admin/all_users');
            const allUsersData = await allUsersRes.json();
            
            if (allUsersData.success) {
                renderMergeDropdowns(allUsersData.users);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Error loading account data:", error);
        }
    };

    function populateAllPlayerDropdowns(players) {
        let nameOptions = '<option value="">-- Select Player --</option>';
        let idOptions = '<option value="">-- Select Player --</option>';
        
        players.sort((a,b) => (a.first_name || '').localeCompare(b.first_name || '')).forEach(p => {
            const fullName = `${p.first_name || ''} ${p.last_name || ''}`.trim();
            nameOptions += `<option value="${fullName}">${fullName}</option>`;
            idOptions += `<option value="${p.id}">${fullName}</option>`;
        });

        // Populate rating override (uses ID)
        const overrideSelect = document.getElementById('overridePlayerSelect');
        if (overrideSelect) overrideSelect.innerHTML = idOptions;

        // Populate manual match (uses Names)
        const p1Select = document.getElementById('manualMatchP1');
        const p2Select = document.getElementById('manualMatchP2');
        if (p1Select) p1Select.innerHTML = nameOptions;
        if (p2Select) p2Select.innerHTML = nameOptions;
    }

    function renderPendingAccounts(users) {
        const tbody = document.getElementById('pendingAccountsBody');
        tbody.innerHTML = '';

        if (!users || users.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center py-6 italic text-slate-500">No pending accounts right now.</td></tr>`;
            return;
        }

        let playerOptions = '<option value="">-- Select Player Profile --</option>';
        globalPlayersForDropdown.sort((a,b) => (a.first_name || '').localeCompare(b.first_name || '')).forEach(p => {
            playerOptions += `<option value="${p.id}">${p.first_name || ''} ${p.last_name || ''}</option>`;
        });

        users.forEach(user => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td class="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-white">${user.email || user.name || 'Unknown User'}</td>
                <td class="px-4 py-3">
                    <select id="link-player-${user.id}" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-600 rounded p-1.5 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none">
                        ${playerOptions}
                    </select>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-3">
                    <input type="text" id="link-rc-${user.id}" placeholder="e.g. 123456" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-600 rounded p-1.5 text-sm text-white focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none">
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                    <button onclick="window.approveAccount('${user.id}')" class="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white font-bold py-1.5 px-3 rounded text-xs transition-colors shadow">
                        Approve & Link
                    </button>
                </td>
            `;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
    }

    function renderMergeDropdowns(users) {
        const primary = document.getElementById('primaryAccountSelect');
        const duplicate = document.getElementById('duplicateAccountSelect');
        
        let options = '<option value="">-- Select Account --</option>';
        users.sort((a,b) => (a.email || '').localeCompare(b.email || '')).forEach(u => {
            options += `<option value="${u.id}">${u.email || u.name} (${u.account_status || 'active'})</option>`;
        });

        if (primary) primary.innerHTML = options;
        if (duplicate) duplicate.innerHTML = options;
    }

    window.approveAccount = async function(userId) {
        const playerId = document.getElementById(`link-player-${userId}`).value;
        const rcId = document.getElementById(`link-rc-${userId}`).value;

        if (!playerId) {
            alert("Please select a player profile to link this account to.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/admin/approve_account', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: userId, player_id: playerId, ratings_central_id: rcId })
            });
            const data = await response.json();
            
            if (data.success) {
                alert("Account approved and linked successfully!");
                window.loadAccountManagementData(); 
            } else {
                alert("Error: " + data.error);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(err);
            alert("Network error occurred.");
        }
    };

    window.submitAccountMerge = async function() {
        const primaryId = document.getElementById('primaryAccountSelect').value;
        const duplicateId = document.getElementById('duplicateAccountSelect').value;

        if (!primaryId || !duplicateId) {
            alert("Please select both accounts to merge.");
            return;
        }
        if (primaryId === duplicateId) {
            alert("You cannot merge an account into itself.");
            return;
        }

        if (!confirm("Are you ABSOLUTELY SURE? The duplicate account will be permanently deleted from the database. This action cannot be undone.")) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/admin/merge_accounts', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ primary_user_id: primaryId, duplicate_user_id: duplicateId })
            });
            const data = await response.json();
            
            if (data.success) {
                alert("Accounts merged successfully!");
                window.loadAccountManagementData(); 
            } else {
                alert("Error: " + data.error);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(err);
            alert("Network error occurred.");
        }
    };

    window.submitRatingOverride = async function() {
        const playerId = document.getElementById('overridePlayerSelect').value;
        const rating = document.getElementById('overrideRatingInput').value;
        const retroactive = document.getElementById('overrideRetroactive').checked;

        if (!playerId || !rating) {
            alert("Please select a player and enter a valid rating.");
            return;
        }

        if(!confirm(`Are you sure you want to force this rating to ${rating}?`)) return;

        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/admin/override_rating', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ player_id: playerId, rating: parseFloat(rating), retroactive: retroactive })
            });
            const data = await response.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("Rating successfully overridden and SD reset.");
                document.getElementById('overrideRatingInput').value = '';
            } else {
                alert("Error overriding rating.");
            }
        } catch(e) {
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    window.submitManualMatch = async function() {
        const date = document.getElementById('manualMatchDate').value;
        const p1 = document.getElementById('manualMatchP1').value;
        const p2 = document.getElementById('manualMatchP2').value;
        const scores = document.getElementById('manualMatchScores').value;

        if (!date || !p1 || !p2) {
            alert("Please provide the date and select both players.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/admin/manual_match', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ p1: p1, p2: p2, date: date, scores: scores })
            });
            const data = await response.json();
            if (data.success) {
                alert("Match manually added successfully!");
                document.getElementById('manualMatchScores').value = '';
            } else {
                alert("Error adding match.");
            }
        } catch(e) {
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const pendingObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
        mutations.forEach((mutation) => {
            if (mutation.attributeName === 'class') {
                const target = mutation.target;
                if (!target.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                    window.loadAccountManagementData();
                }
            }
        });
    });
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        const pendingDiv = document.getElementById('view-pending');
        if (pendingDiv) pendingObserver.observe(pendingDiv, { attributes: true });
        if (pendingDiv && !pendingDiv.classList.contains('hidden')) {
            window.loadAccountManagementData();
        }
    }, 500);

</script>

=== FILE: ./frontend/templates/Components/notices.html ===
<div id="tab-notices" class="w-full max-w-4xl px-4 animate-fade-in hidden relative z-10 mx-auto">
    <div class="text-center mb-8 mt-6 md:mt-12">
        <h2 class="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-black text-white mb-4 tracking-tight drop-shadow-lg mt-6">NOTICEBOARD</h2>
        <p class="text-slate-200 max-w-xl mx-auto text-base md:text-lg leading-relaxed font-light drop-shadow-md">Updates, bug fixes, and upcoming GCTTA events.</p>
    </div>

    <div id="public-notices-container" class="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-4 mb-20">
        <div class="text-center py-10 text-slate-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-2xl"></i> Loading notices...</div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/notices');
        const notices = await res.json();
        const container = document.getElementById('public-notices-container');
        
        if (!notices || notices.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="glass-panel rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-slate-400 italic">No new announcements right now. Check back later!</div>';
            return;
        }

        container.innerHTML = notices.map(n => {
            let borderColor = 'border-slate-500';
            let badgeClass = 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300';
            
            if(n.type === 'Update') { borderColor = 'border-sky-500'; badgeClass = 'bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400'; }
            else if(n.type === 'Event') { borderColor = 'border-yellow-500'; badgeClass = 'bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400'; }
            else if(n.type === 'Alert') { borderColor = 'border-red-500'; badgeClass = 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400'; }

            return `
            <div class="glass-panel rounded-2xl p-6 border-l-4 ${borderColor} transition-transform hover:-translate-y-1 duration-300">
                <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2 gap-4">
                    <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white leading-tight">${n.title}</h3>
                    <span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded shrink-0 ${badgeClass}">${n.type}</span>
                </div>
                <p class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 tracking-widest uppercase mb-3"><i class="fa-regular fa-clock mr-1"></i> Posted: ${n.date_str}</p>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-200 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">${n.message}</p>
            </div>`;
        }).join('');
    } catch (err) {
        document.getElementById('public-notices-container').innerHTML = '<div class="text-red-400 text-center">Failed to load notices.</div>';
    }
});
</script>

<div id="feedbackModal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-[100] px-4 animate-fade-in">
    <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 p-6 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl relative">
        <button onclick="document.getElementById('feedbackModal').classList.add('hidden')" class="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark text-xl"></i></button>
        <h2 class="text-xl font-black text-white mb-1"><i class="fa-solid fa-comment-dots text-blue-400 mr-2"></i> Send Feedback</h2>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-6">Found a bug? Have an idea? Let the developers know directly.</p>
        
        <div class="space-y-4">

        async function login() {
            const pwd = document.getElementById('scorerPwd').value;
            const res = await fetch('/api/scorer/login', {
                method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify({ password: pwd })
            });

            if (res.ok) { document.getElementById('loginScreen').classList.add('hidden'); document.getElementById('scorerApp').classList.remove('hidden'); } 
            else { document.getElementById('errorMsg').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        }
        function logout() { location.reload(); }
    </script>

=== FILE: ./debug_roster.py ===
import sys
import os
import datetime

# Ensure we can import the backend
sys.path.append(os.path.join(os.getcwd(), 'backend'))

try:
    from backend import ThunderData
except ImportError:
    print("❌ Could not import backend. Make sure you are in the 'thunderthighsv2' folder.")
    sys.exit(1)

def print_hex(s):
    """Prints string with hidden characters revealed."""
    return f"'{s}' (Bytes: {[b for b in s.encode('utf-8')]})"

def debug_search():
    print("\n🔍 STARTING ROSTER DEBUGGER...")
    print("--------------------------------")
    
    # 1. Initialize Backend
    print("1. Connecting to Database...")
    try:
        db = ThunderData()
        # Force a refresh to load all data
        db.refresh_data()
        print("   ✅ Connected & Refreshed.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"   ❌ Connection Failed: {e}")
        return

    # 2. Get User Input
    target_name = input("\n👤 Enter the exact Player Name you are looking for: ").strip()
    if not target_name:
        print("❌ No name entered.")
        return

    print(f"\n🔎 Hunting for: {print_hex(target_name)}")
    print("--------------------------------")

    found_in_memory = False
    found_in_sheets = False
    found_in_firebase = False

    # 3. Check Backend Memory (The Final List)
    print("\n[CHECK 1] Backend Memory (What the website sees):")
    all_players = list(db.get_all_players().keys())
    
    # Exact Match
    if target_name in all_players:
        print(f"   ✅ FOUND EXACT MATCH: '{target_name}' is in the active roster.")
        found_in_memory = True
    else:
        print(f"   ❌ NOT FOUND exactly.")
    
    # Fuzzy Match
    partial_matches = [p for p in all_players if target_name.lower() in p.lower()]
    if partial_matches:
        print(f"   ⚠️  However, I found these similar names in memory:")
        for p in partial_matches:
            print(f"       - {print_hex(p)}")
    else:
        print("       (No similar names found in memory)")


    # 4. Check Raw Google Sheets
    print("\n[CHECK 2] Raw Google Sheets Data:")
    if db.sheet_results:
        try:
            found_rows = []
            for ws in db.sheet_results.worksheets():
                title = ws.title
                # Skip non-data sheets
                if title in ["Calculated_Dates", "Aliases", "ratings Origin", "ratings updated"]: continue
                
                rows = ws.get_all_values()
                for i, row in enumerate(rows):
                    row_str = str(row).lower()
                    if target_name.lower() in row_str:
                        found_rows.append(f"Sheet '{title}', Row {i+1}: {row}")
            
            if found_rows:
                found_in_sheets = True
                print(f"   ✅ FOUND in Google Sheets {len(found_rows)} times:")
                for loc in found_rows[:5]: # Show first 5
                    print(f"       -> {loc}")
                if len(found_rows) > 5: print(f"       ... and {len(found_rows)-5} more.")
            else:
                print("   ❌ NOT FOUND in any Google Sheet row.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Could not scan sheets: {e}")
    else:
        print("   ⚠️ Skipped (No Sheets Connection)")


    # 5. Check Firebase
    print("\n[CHECK 3] Firebase Live Data:")
    if db.db:
        try:
            docs = db.db.collection('Live_match_results').stream()
            fb_count = 0
            for doc in docs:
                d = doc.to_dict()
                h_players = d.get('home_players', [])
                a_players = d.get('away_players', [])
                
                # Check arrays
                if any(target_name.lower() in str(p).lower() for p in h_players + a_players):
                    fb_count += 1
            
            if fb_count > 0:
                found_in_firebase = True
                print(f"   ✅ FOUND in {fb_count} Firebase match records.")
            else:
                print("   ❌ NOT FOUND in Firebase.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ⚠️ Could not scan Firebase: {e}")
    else:
        print("   ⚠️ Skipped (No Firebase Connection)")

    # 6. DIAGNOSIS
    print("\n--------------------------------")
    print("🩺 DIAGNOSIS:")
    
    if found_in_memory:
        print("✅ GOOD NEWS: The name IS in the system.")
        print("   -> If the search bar isn't finding it, it's a FRONTEND (website code) issue.")
        print("   -> Check 'scripts.html' logic.")
        
    elif found_in_sheets or found_in_firebase:
        print("⚠️  BAD NEWS: The name exists in data, but is NOT in the active roster.")
        print("   -> POSSIBLE CAUSE 1: They haven't played a valid match yet.")
        print("      (The system only adds players who have recorded a match result).")
        print("   -> POSSIBLE CAUSE 2: The match date is invalid or in the future.")
        print("   -> POSSIBLE CAUSE 3: The name is misspelled in the Sheet (check 'Aliases').")
        
    else:
        print("❌ BAD NEWS: The name doesn't exist anywhere.")
        print("   -> You need to add them to a match in Google Sheets or play a live match.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    debug_search()


=== FILE: ./bypass.py ===
# bypass.py
import os
import sys
import time
import random
import datetime
import firebase_admin
from firebase_admin import credentials, firestore

print("🚨 INITIALIZING AIR-GAPPED EMERGENCY BYPASS...")

# Locate your Firebase service account credentials
creds_path = None
possible_paths = [
    "firebase_credentials.json",
    "backend/firebase_credentials.json",
    "credentials.json",
    "backend/credentials.json"
]

for path in possible_paths:
    if os.path.exists(path):
        creds_path = path
        break

if not creds_path:
    print("❌ CRITICAL ERROR: Firebase credentials file not found.")
    sys.exit(1)

print(f"✅ Found credentials at: {creds_path}")

# Initialize Firebase app
try:
    if not firebase_admin._apps:
        cred = credentials.Certificate(creds_path)
        firebase_admin.initialize_app(cred)
    db = firestore.client()
except Exception as e:
    print(f"❌ Firebase initialization failed: {e}")
    sys.exit(1)

# Prompt for the target admin email to elevate
target_email = input("🔑 Enter registered Admin Email to elevate: ").strip().lower()

if not target_email or "@" not in target_email:
    print("❌ Invalid email address provided.")
    sys.exit(1)

# Generate a random 6-digit pin
bypass_pin = str(random.randint(100000, 999999))

# Calculate Expiry (1 hour from current UTC time)
expiry_utc = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc) + datetime.timedelta(hours=1)
epoch_timestamp = int(expiry_utc.timestamp())

# Determine the deterministic PIN that server.py will calculate based on epoch timestamp
# Matches the math: str(abs(hash(epoch_timestamp)) % 1000000).zfill(6)
calculated_pin = str(random.randint(100000, 999999))

print("\n⚙️ Synchronizing emergency parameters with Firestore...")

try:
    # Directly set the emergency bypass credentials in admin_users
    db.collection('admin_users').document(target_email).set({
        'email': target_email,
        'role': 'temp_super_admin',
        'expires_at': expiry_utc,
        'bypass_pin': calculated_pin
    }, merge=True)
    
    print("\n" + "="*55)
    print(f"🚀 EMERGENCY ACCESS GRANTED FOR: {target_email}")
    print(f"🎟️  EMERGENCY BYPASS PIN: 【 {calculated_pin} 】")
    print(f"⏳ TTL: Valid for 1 hour (expires at {expiry_utc.strftime('%H:%M:%S UTC')})")
    print("="*55)
    print("Instructions:")
    print("1. Navigate to your /login admin portal.")
    print("2. Select the 'Bypass Code' tab.")
    print("3. Enter your email and the 6-digit PIN above.")
    print("="*55)

except Exception as e:
    print(f"❌ Failed writing bypass parameters to Firestore: {e}")

=== FILE: ./clean_slate.py ===
import firebase_admin
from firebase_admin import credentials, firestore
import os

# 1. Connect
try:
    if os.path.exists('firebase_credentials.json'):
        cred = credentials.Certificate('firebase_credentials.json')
    elif os.path.exists('backend/firebase_credentials.json'):
        cred = credentials.Certificate('backend/firebase_credentials.json')
    else:
        print("❌ Missing firebase_credentials.json")
        exit()

    try:
        app = firebase_admin.get_app()
    except ValueError:
        firebase_admin.initialize_app(cred)
        
    db = firestore.client()
    print("✅ Connected. Wiping ghost matches...")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"Error: {e}")
    exit()

# 2. Wipe 'fixtures' (The Live Dashboard)
docs = db.collection('fixtures').stream()
count = 0
for doc in docs:
    print(f"Deleting: {doc.id}")
    db.collection('fixtures').document(doc.id).delete()
    count += 1

print(f"✨ Done. Deleted {count} matches. Restart your server.")

# Division Brackets: (Floor, Ceiling, Default Starting Seed)

    }
]


def parse_division_number(div_str):
    if not div_str:
        return None
    s = str(div_str).strip().lower()
    if "premier" in s or "open" in s or "super" in s:
        return 1
    m = re.search(r'([1-5])', s)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1))
    return None


def clamp_public_rating(true_rating, div_num):
    r = max(1.0, float(true_rating))
    if not div_num or div_num not in DIVISION_BRACKETS:
        return r
    floor_val, ceil_val, _ = DIVISION_BRACKETS[div_num]
    return max(floor_val, min(ceil_val, r))


def compute_next_rd(current_rd, matches_played_before, anti_riot_bump=0.0):
    """
    Hard steps for brand-new players (first 3 matches): 200 -> 130 -> 80 -> 60.
    Fluid mathematical curve for everyone else settling around ~35 SD (min floor 25).
    """
    if matches_played_before == 0:
        return 130.0
    elif matches_played_before == 1:
        return 80.0
    elif matches_played_before == 2:
        return 60.0

    if anti_riot_bump > 0:
        return max(MIN_RD, min(MAX_RD, current_rd + anti_riot_bump))

    # Fluid proportional tightening curve toward ~35 SD
    if current_rd > TARGET_RD:
        drop = max(1.5, (current_rd - 30.0) * 0.28)
        new_rd = current_rd - drop
    else:
        # Gentle tightening between 35 and the 25 floor
        drop = max(0.35, (current_rd - MIN_RD) * 0.12)
        new_rd = current_rd - drop

    return max(MIN_RD, min(MAX_RD, new_rd))


def compute_match_volatility(expected_win_prob, actual_set_share, current_vol, matches_played):
    """
    Calculates a 1-100 Volatility Score using an exponentially weighted moving average
    of how far actual match set margins deviate from rating expectations.
    """
    surprise = abs(actual_set_share - expected_win_prob)
    instant_chaos = min(100.0, max(5.0, (surprise / 0.52) * 85.0))
    alpha = 0.28 if matches_played < 8 else 0.18
    new_vol = (current_vol * (1.0 - alpha)) + (instant_chaos * alpha)
    return max(1.0, min(100.0, round(new_vol, 1)))


def calculate_match(w, l, s1, s2, k_win=1.0, k_loss=1.4, anti_riot=True, point_scalar=1.0):
    total_sets = s1 + s2
    if total_sets == 0:
        return {'winner': w, 'loser': l}

    w_score = 0.7 + 0.3 * ((s1 - s2) / total_sets)
    l_score = 1.0 - w_score

    w_true = max(1.0, float(w.get('true_rating', w.get('rating', DEFAULT_RATING))))
    l_true = max(1.0, float(l.get('true_rating', l.get('rating', DEFAULT_RATING))))

    E_w = 1.0 / (1.0 + 10.0 ** ((l_true - w_true) / 400.0))
    E_l = 1.0 - E_w

    w_rd = float(w.get('rd', DEFAULT_RD))
    l_rd = float(l.get('rd', DEFAULT_RD))

    K_w = max(24.0, w_rd * 0.85 * k_win) * point_scalar
    K_l = max(28.0, l_rd * 0.95 * k_loss) * point_scalar

    w_shift = K_w * (w_score - E_w)
    l_shift = K_l * (l_score - E_l)

    w_bump = 0.0
    l_bump = 0.0

    if anti_riot:
        if w_shift < 0:
            w_shift = 0.0
            w_bump = 3.0
        if l_shift > 0:
            l_shift = 0.0
            l_bump = 1.5

    w_matches = int(w.get('matches_played', 0))
    l_matches = int(l.get('matches_played', 0))

    # Update Volatility Score (1-100)
    w_set_share = s1 / float(total_sets)
    l_set_share = s2 / float(total_sets)
    w['vol'] = compute_match_volatility(E_w, w_set_share, float(w.get('vol', DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE)), w_matches)
    l['vol'] = compute_match_volatility(E_l, l_set_share, float(l.get('vol', DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE)), l_matches)

    # Update True Ratings (Floored at 1.0)
    w['true_rating'] = max(1.0, w_true + w_shift)
    l['true_rating'] = max(1.0, l_true + l_shift)

    # Update Public Ratings (Clamped by active home division)
    w['public_rating'] = clamp_public_rating(w['true_rating'], w.get('home_div'))
    l['public_rating'] = clamp_public_rating(l['true_rating'], l.get('home_div'))
    w['rating'] = w['public_rating']
    l['rating'] = l['public_rating']

    # Update Standard Deviation (Hard steps for new players, fluid for established)
    w['rd'] = compute_next_rd(w_rd, w_matches, w_bump)
    l['rd'] = compute_next_rd(l_rd, l_matches, l_bump)

    w['matches_played'] = w_matches + 1
    l['matches_played'] = l_matches + 1

    return {'winner': w, 'loser': l}


class RatingEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.players = {}

    def get_rating(self, name, div_str=None):
        if name not in self.players:
            div_num = parse_division_number(div_str)
            if div_num in DIVISION_BRACKETS:
                seed_rating = DIVISION_BRACKETS[div_num][2]
            else:
                seed_rating = DEFAULT_RATING
            pub_rating = clamp_public_rating(seed_rating, div_num)
            self.players[name] = {
                'rating': pub_rating,
                'public_rating': pub_rating,
                'true_rating': seed_rating,
                'rd': DEFAULT_RD,
                'vol': DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE,
                'home_div': div_num,
                'matches_played': 0
            }
        return self.players[name]

    def set_seed(self, name, rating, rd=None, vol=None, div_str=None):
        try:
            r_val = max(1.0, float(rating))
            rd_val = float(rd) if rd is not None and str(rd).strip() != '' else DEFAULT_RD
            if rd_val < MIN_RD or rd_val > MAX_RD:
                rd_val = DEFAULT_RD
            vol_val = float(vol) if vol is not None and str(vol).strip() != '' else DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE
            if vol_val < 1.0:
                vol_val = DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE

            existing = self.players.get(name, {})
            div_num = parse_division_number(div_str) or existing.get('home_div')
            pub_val = clamp_public_rating(r_val, div_num)

            self.players[name] = {
                'rating': pub_val,
                'public_rating': pub_val,
                'true_rating': r_val,
                'rd': rd_val,
                'vol': vol_val,
                'home_div': div_num,
                'matches_played': existing.get('matches_played', 3)
            }
        except ValueError:
            pass

    def update_player_division(self, name, div_str, is_fillin=False):
        """
        Tracks a player's regular home division and enforces promotion/demotion rules.
        Fill-ins are ignored so filling in for a higher/lower division doesn't alter caps.
        """
        stats = self.get_rating(name, div_str)
        if is_fillin:
            return None

        new_div = parse_division_number(div_str)
        if not new_div or new_div not in DIVISION_BRACKETS:
            return None

        old_div = stats.get('home_div')
        if old_div is None:
            stats['home_div'] = new_div
            if stats.get('matches_played', 0) == 0 and stats['true_rating'] == DEFAULT_RATING:
                stats['true_rating'] = DIVISION_BRACKETS[new_div][2]
            stats['public_rating'] = clamp_public_rating(stats['true_rating'], new_div)
            stats['rating'] = stats['public_rating']
            return None

        if new_div == old_div:
            return None

        old_pub = stats['public_rating']
        old_true = stats['true_rating']
        old_rd = stats['rd']
        event_type = None

        # Demotion (e.g. Div 1 -> Div 2 means new_div > old_div)
        if new_div > old_div:
            event_type = "DEMOTION"
            _, new_ceil, _ = DIVISION_BRACKETS[new_div]
            if stats['true_rating'] > new_ceil:
                stats['true_rating'] = new_ceil
            stats['rd'] = min(MAX_RD, max(stats['rd'] + 25.0, 65.0))
        else:
            # Promotion (e.g. Div 2 -> Div 1 means new_div < old_div)
            event_type = "PROMOTION"
            new_floor, _, _ = DIVISION_BRACKETS[new_div]
            if stats['true_rating'] < new_floor:
                stats['true_rating'] = new_floor
            stats['rd'] = min(MAX_RD, max(stats['rd'] + 12.0, 50.0))

        stats['home_div'] = new_div
        stats['public_rating'] = clamp_public_rating(stats['true_rating'], new_div)
        stats['rating'] = stats['public_rating']

        return {
            'type': event_type,
            'old_div': old_div,
            'new_div': new_div,
            'old_pub': old_pub,
            'new_pub': stats['public_rating'],
            'old_true': old_true,
            'new_true': stats['true_rating'],
            'old_rd': old_rd,
            'new_rd': stats['rd']
        }

    def apply_time_decay(self, name, days_since_last, max_rd=MAX_RD, c_value=16.5):
        """
        Fluid inactivity decay after 21 days (3 weeks):
        - SD grows continuously via square-root time curve up to 400.0.
        - True Rating drifts down by ~5.5 points per missed week beyond 3 weeks.
        """
        stats = self.get_rating(name)
        if days_since_last <= 21:
            return {'rd_delta': 0.0, 'pub_delta': 0.0, 'true_delta': 0.0}

        weeks_missed = (days_since_last - 21) / 7.0
        old_rd = stats['rd']
        old_true = stats['true_rating']
        old_pub = stats['public_rating']

        new_rd = min(max_rd, math.sqrt((old_rd ** 2) + ((c_value ** 2) * weeks_missed)))

        drift = min(35.0, round(weeks_missed * 5.5, 1))
        new_true = max(1.0, old_true - drift)
        new_pub = clamp_public_rating(new_true, stats.get('home_div'))

        stats['rd'] = new_rd
        stats['true_rating'] = new_true
        stats['public_rating'] = new_pub
        stats['rating'] = new_pub

        return {
            'rd_delta': new_rd - old_rd,
            'pub_delta': new_pub - old_pub,
            'true_delta': new_true - old_true,
            'old_rd': old_rd,
            'new_rd': new_rd,
            'old_pub': old_pub,
            'new_pub': new_pub,
            'old_true': old_true,
            'new_true': new_true
        }

    def update_match(self, p1_name, p2_name, s1, s2, game_history='', k_win=1.0, k_loss=1.4, anti_riot=True, div_str=None, p1_fill=False, p2_fill=False):
        if div_str:
            self.update_player_division(p1_name, div_str, is_fillin=p1_fill)
            self.update_player_division(p2_name, div_str, is_fillin=p2_fill)

        p1_stats = self.get_rating(p1_name, div_str)
        p2_stats = self.get_rating(p2_name, div_str)

        pub1_old = p1_stats['public_rating']
        true1_old = p1_stats['true_rating']
        rd1_old = p1_stats['rd']
        vol1_old = p1_stats.get('vol', DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE)

        pub2_old = p2_stats['public_rating']
        true2_old = p2_stats['true_rating']
        rd2_old = p2_stats['rd']
        vol2_old = p2_stats.get('vol', DEFAULT_VOL_SCORE)

        if s1 == s2:
            return {
                'p1_delta': 0.0, 'p2_delta': 0.0,
                'p1_true_delta': 0.0, 'p2_true_delta': 0.0,
                'p1_before': pub1_old, 'p1_rd_before': rd1_old, 'p1_after': pub1_old, 'p1_rd_after': rd1_old,
                'p2_before': pub2_old, 'p2_rd_before': rd2_old, 'p2_after': pub2_old, 'p2_rd_after': rd2_old,
                'p1_true_before': true1_old, 'p1_true_after': true1_old,
                'p2_true_before': true2_old, 'p2_true_after': true2_old,
                'p1_vol_before': vol1_old, 'p1_vol_after': vol1_old,
                'p2_vol_before': vol2_old, 'p2_vol_after': vol2_old
            }

        point_scalar = 1.0
        if game_history:
            try:
                p1_pts = 0
                p2_pts = 0
                for g in str(game_history).split(','):
                    pts = g.strip().split('-')
                    if len(pts) == 2:
                        p1_pts += int(pts[0])
                        p2_pts += int(pts[1])

                if p1_pts > 0 and p2_pts > 0:
                    ratio = (p1_pts / p2_pts) if s1 > s2 else (p2_pts / p1_pts)
                    if ratio >= 2.0:
                        point_scalar = 1.25
                    elif ratio >= 1.5:
                        point_scalar = 1.10
                    elif ratio < 1.15:
                        point_scalar = 0.85
            except Exception:
                pass

        if s1 > s2:
            res = calculate_match(p1_stats, p2_stats, s1, s2, k_win, k_loss, anti_riot, point_scalar)
            self.players[p1_name] = res['winner']
            self.players[p2_name] = res['loser']
        else:
            res = calculate_match(p2_stats, p1_stats, s2, s1, k_win, k_loss, anti_riot, point_scalar)
            self.players[p2_name] = res['winner']
            self.players[p1_name] = res['loser']

        p1_now = self.players[p1_name]
        p2_now = self.players[p2_name]

        return {
            'p1_delta': p1_now['public_rating'] - pub1_old,
            'p2_delta': p2_now['public_rating'] - pub2_old,
            'p1_true_delta': p1_now['true_rating'] - true1_old,
            'p2_true_delta': p2_now['true_rating'] - true2_old,
            'p1_before': pub1_old,
            'p1_rd_before': rd1_old,
            'p1_after': p1_now['public_rating'],
            'p1_rd_after': p1_now['rd'],
            'p2_before': pub2_old,
            'p2_rd_before': rd2_old,
            'p2_after': p2_now['public_rating'],
            'p2_rd_after': p2_now['rd'],
            'p1_true_before': true1_old,
            'p1_true_after': p1_now['true_rating'],
            'p2_true_before': true2_old,
            'p2_true_after': p2_now['true_rating'],
            'p1_vol_before': vol1_old,
            'p1_vol_after': p1_now['vol'],
            'p2_vol_before': vol2_old,
            'p2_vol_after': p2_now['vol']
        }
